// Configuration

var SHEET_ID = "1mZlKmBLoLXwYNeIBcqDvE1NZgd1KQ3rXYDh_UfxLApI";
var SESSION_DURATION = 30 * 60 * 1000; // 30 minutes in milliseconds
var MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS = 5; // Max attempts per 15 minutes
var ATTEMPT_WINDOW = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes in milliseconds

// ============================================
// ACCOUNT TRANSACTION HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get account transactions by docType and docNo
function handleGetAccountTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docType = e.parameter.docType;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docType || !docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocType and DocNo are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get account transactions
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!accountTransactionSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", entries: [] };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  // Build COA description lookup map (only GL accounts)
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  var coaDescMap = {};
  if (coaSheet) {
    var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      var acType = String(coaData[i][1] || '');
      var acCode = String(coaData[i][0] || '');
      var desc = coaData[i][2] || '';
      // Only map GL accounts (posting level), skip groups and headers
      if (acCode && acType === 'GL') {
        coaDescMap[acCode] = desc;
      }
    }
  }
  
  var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var entries = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] && data[i][0].toString() == docType.toString() && data[i][1] && data[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
      var acDocNo = String(data[i][3] || '');
      var accountDesc = coaDescMap[acDocNo] || '';
      var amount = parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0;
      
      // -ve AMOUNT = Debit, +ve AMOUNT = Credit
      var debit = amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0;
      var credit = amount > 0 ? amount : 0;
      
      entries.push({
        docSrNo: data[i][2] || 0,
        acDocNo: acDocNo,
        accountDescription: accountDesc,
        subledgerDocNo: data[i][4] || '',
        docDate: data[i][5] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][5]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        valDate: data[i][6] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        narration: data[i][7] || '',
        amount: amount,
        debit: Math.round(debit * 100) / 100,
        credit: Math.round(credit * 100) / 100,
        balanceAmount: parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0,
        refNo: data[i][10] || '',
        refDate: data[i][11] || '',
        createdBy: data[i][12] || '',
        createdTs: data[i][13] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", entries: entries };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists with proper headers
function ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("AccountTransaction");
    sheet.appendRow(["DOCTYPE", "DOCNO", "DOCSRNO", "AC_DOCNO", "SUBLEDGER_DOCNO", "DOCDATE", "VALDATE", "NARRATION", "AMOUNT", "BAL_AMOUNT", "REF_NO", "REF_DATE", "CREATED_BY", "CREATED_TS"]);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Post SI to AccountTransaction (Sales Accounting Entry)
function handlePostSIToAccountTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if accounting entries already exist for this document
  var existingSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (existingSheet) {
    var existingData = existingSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
      if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString() == 'SI' && existingData[i][1] && existingData[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        var alreadyResult = { status: "error", message: "Accounting entries already exist for this document. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(alreadyResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(alreadyResult);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get sale header to find date and amounts
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  if (!salesHeaderSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SalesHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: salesHeaderData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: salesHeaderData[i][1],
        date: salesHeaderData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        customer: salesHeaderData[i][3] || '',
        grossAmount: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][5]) || 0,
        discountAmount: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][7]) || 0,
        vat: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][8]) || 0,
        net: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][9]) || 0
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Sale not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read system parameters for account codes
  var systemParamSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SystemParameters");
  var systemParams = {};
  if (systemParamSheet) {
    var paramData = systemParamSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < paramData.length; i++) {
      if (paramData[i][0]) {
        systemParams[String(paramData[i][0]).trim()] = paramData[i][1] || '';
      }
    }
  }
  
  var accountsReceivableCode = systemParams['AccountsReceivableCode'] || '';
  var salesAccount = systemParams['SalesAccount'] || '';
  var outputVATAccount = systemParams['OutputVATAccount'] || '';
  var salesDiscountAccount = systemParams['SalesDiscountAccount'] || '';
  
  if (!accountsReceivableCode || !salesAccount) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "System parameters AccountsReceivableCode and SalesAccount are not configured" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  var netAmt = header.net || 0;
  var vatAmt = header.vat || 0;
  var discAmt = header.discountAmount || 0;
  var customerAmt = netAmt + vatAmt; // Total receivable = Net + VAT
  
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTs = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  var docSrNo = 1;
  
  // Entry 1: Debit Accounts Receivable (Customer) - Negative amount
  // Narration includes customer code
  accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
    'SI', docNo, docSrNo++, accountsReceivableCode, header.customer,
    header.date, header.date, 'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Customer ' + header.customer,
    -customerAmt, 0, '', '', validation.userName, createdTs
  ]);
  
  // Entry 2: Credit Sales Account (Revenue) - Positive amount
  accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
    'SI', docNo, docSrNo++, salesAccount, '',
    header.date, header.date, 'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Sales Revenue',
    netAmt, 0, '', '', validation.userName, createdTs
  ]);
  
  // Entry 3: Credit Output VAT Account - Positive amount (if VAT > 0)
  if (vatAmt > 0 && outputVATAccount) {
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'SI', docNo, docSrNo++, outputVATAccount, '',
      header.date, header.date, 'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Output VAT',
      vatAmt, 0, '', '', validation.userName, createdTs
    ]);
  }
  
  // Entry 4: Debit Sales Discount Account - Negative amount (if discount > 0)
  if (discAmt > 0 && salesDiscountAccount) {
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'SI', docNo, docSrNo++, salesDiscountAccount, '',
      header.date, header.date, 'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Discount',
      -discAmt, 0, '', '', validation.userName, createdTs
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Sales accounting entries created successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel SI AccountTransaction Post (Delete entries)
function handleCancelSIAccountTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete from AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!accountTransactionSheet) {
    var result = { status: "success", message: "No accounting entries to cancel" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  }
  
  var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var deleted = false;
  
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (data[i][0] && data[i][0].toString() == 'SI' && data[i][1] && data[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
      accountTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      deleted = true;
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: deleted ? "Accounting entries cancelled successfully" : "No accounting entries found" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// CHART OF ACCOUNTS (COA) HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all COA accounts
function handleGetCOAGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all accounts from COA sheet
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  if (!coaSheet) {
    // Create sheet if it doesn't exist
    coaSheet = ss.insertSheet("COA");
    coaSheet.appendRow(["AC_CODE", "AC_TYPE", "DESCRIPTION", "LEVEL", "PARENT", "DOCNO", "FIRST_LEVEL", "BALANCE_SHEET_ACCOUNT", "SUBLEDGER_EXISTS", "DOC_SRNO", "CREATED_BY", "CREATED_TS", "REMARKS", "ACTYPE_DOCNO", "DEFAULT_BALANCE_SIGN", "SRNO"]);
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", accounts: [] };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var accounts = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
    if (coaData[i][2]) { // Check if DESCRIPTION exists
      accounts.push({
        acCode: coaData[i][0] || '',
        acType: coaData[i][1] || '',
        description: coaData[i][2] || '',
        level: parseInt(coaData[i][3]) || 1,
        parent: parseInt(coaData[i][4]) || 0,
        docno: parseInt(coaData[i][5]) || 0,
        firstLevel: parseInt(coaData[i][6]) || 0,
        balanceSheetAccount: parseInt(coaData[i][7]) || 0,
        subledgerExists: parseInt(coaData[i][8]) || 0,
        docSrno: parseInt(coaData[i][9]) || 0,
        createdBy: coaData[i][10] || '',
        createdTs: coaData[i][11] || '',
        remarks: coaData[i][12] || '',
        actypeDocno: coaData[i][13] || '',
        defaultBalanceSign: parseInt(coaData[i][14]) || -1,
        srno: parseInt(coaData[i][15]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", accounts: accounts };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new COA account
function handleCreateCOAGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var accountDataJson = e.parameter.accountData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse data
  var accountData;
  try {
    accountData = JSON.parse(accountDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input (AC_TYPE is optional: GL = ledger, blank = group/header)
  if (!accountData.description || !accountData.level) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Description and Level are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // GL (Ledger) accounts must have an Account Code
  var newAcType = accountData.acType || '';
  if (String(newAcType).toUpperCase() === 'GL' && !accountData.acCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account Code is required for Ledger (GL) accounts" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get or create COA sheet
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  if (!coaSheet) {
    coaSheet = ss.insertSheet("COA");
    coaSheet.appendRow(["AC_CODE", "AC_TYPE", "DESCRIPTION", "LEVEL", "PARENT", "DOCNO", "FIRST_LEVEL", "BALANCE_SHEET_ACCOUNT", "SUBLEDGER_EXISTS", "DOC_SRNO", "CREATED_BY", "CREATED_TS", "REMARKS", "ACTYPE_DOCNO", "DEFAULT_BALANCE_SIGN", "SRNO"]);
  }
  
  // Get existing data to calc max DOCNO and SRNO
  var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxDocno = 0;
  var maxSrno = 0;
  
  // Validate account code is not duplicated
  if (accountData.acCode) {
    var acCodeUpper = String(accountData.acCode).toString().toUpperCase();
    for (var ci = 1; ci < coaData.length; ci++) {
      if (coaData[ci][0] && String(coaData[ci][0]).toString().toUpperCase() == acCodeUpper) {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account Code already exists" };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
    }
  }
  
  for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
    if (coaData[i][5]) {
      var docno = parseInt(coaData[i][5]);
      if (docno > maxDocno) maxDocno = docno;
    }
    if (coaData[i][15]) {
      var srno = parseInt(coaData[i][15]);
      if (srno > maxSrno) maxSrno = srno;
    }
  }
  
  var newDocno = maxDocno + 1;
  var newSrno = maxSrno + 1;
  
  // Get current timestamp
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTs = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  // Validate parent exists if specified
  var parent = parseInt(accountData.parent) || 0;
  if (parent > 0) {
    var parentExists = false;
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      if (parseInt(coaData[i][5]) == parent) {
        parentExists = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!parentExists) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Parent account not found" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new account
  coaSheet.appendRow([
    accountData.acCode || '',
    accountData.acType,
    accountData.description,
    parseInt(accountData.level),
    parent,
    newDocno,
    parseInt(accountData.firstLevel) || 0,
    parseInt(accountData.balanceSheetAccount) || 0,
    parseInt(accountData.subledgerExists) || 0,
    parseInt(accountData.docSrno) || 0,
    validation.userName,
    createdTs,
    accountData.remarks || '',
    accountData.actypeDocno || '',
    parseInt(accountData.defaultBalanceSign) || -1,
    newSrno
  ]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Account created successfully", docno: newDocno, srno: newSrno };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing COA account
function handleUpdateCOAGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var accountDataJson = e.parameter.accountData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse data
  var accountData;
  try {
    accountData = JSON.parse(accountDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!accountData.srno) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SRNO is required for update" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  if (!coaSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "COA sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var found = false;
  var rowIndex = -1;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
    if (parseInt(coaData[i][15]) == parseInt(accountData.srno)) {
      found = true;
      rowIndex = i + 1; // 1-based for sheet operations
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Preserve original DOCNO, SRNO, createdBy, createdTs
  var originalDocno = coaData[rowIndex - 1][5];
  var originalSrno = coaData[rowIndex - 1][15];
  var originalCreatedBy = coaData[rowIndex - 1][10];
  var originalCreatedTs = coaData[rowIndex - 1][11];
  
  // GL (Ledger) accounts must have an Account Code
  var acTypeToSave = accountData.hasOwnProperty('acType') ? (accountData.acType || '') : (coaData[rowIndex - 1][1] || '');
  if (String(acTypeToSave).toUpperCase() === 'GL' && !accountData.acCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account Code is required for Ledger (GL) accounts" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate account code is not duplicated (excluding this account itself)
  if (accountData.acCode) {
    var acCodeUpper = String(accountData.acCode).toString().toUpperCase();
    for (var ci = 1; ci < coaData.length; ci++) {
      if (coaData[ci][0] && String(coaData[ci][0]).toString().toUpperCase() == acCodeUpper &&
          parseInt(coaData[ci][15]) != parseInt(accountData.srno)) {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account Code already exists" };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Update the row
  coaSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, 16).setValues([[
    accountData.acCode || '',
    // AC_TYPE: GL = ledger, blank = group. Allow clearing to blank (hasOwnProperty check).
    accountData.hasOwnProperty('acType') ? accountData.acType : coaData[rowIndex - 1][1],
    accountData.description || coaData[rowIndex - 1][2],
    parseInt(accountData.level) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][3]) || 1,
    parseInt(accountData.parent) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][4]) || 0,
    originalDocno,
    parseInt(accountData.firstLevel) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][6]) || 0,
    parseInt(accountData.balanceSheetAccount) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][7]) || 0,
    parseInt(accountData.subledgerExists) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][8]) || 0,
    parseInt(accountData.docSrno) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][9]) || 0,
    originalCreatedBy,
    originalCreatedTs,
    accountData.remarks || '',
    accountData.actypeDocno || '',
    parseInt(accountData.defaultBalanceSign) || parseInt(coaData[rowIndex - 1][14]) || -1,
    originalSrno
  ]]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Account updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete COA account
function handleDeleteCOAGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var srno = e.parameter.srno;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!srno) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SRNO is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  if (!coaSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "COA sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var found = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
    if (parseInt(coaData[i][15]) == parseInt(srno)) {
      coaSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Account deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// MAIN ENDPOINTS
// ============================================

// POST endpoint for secure login authentication
function doPost(e) {
  try {
    // Try to parse as JSON first, fall back to URL-encoded form data
    var requestData;
    try {
      requestData = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents);
    } catch (jsonError) {
      // If JSON parsing fails, use e.parameter (URL-encoded form data)
      requestData = {};
      for (var key in e.parameter) {
        if (e.parameter.hasOwnProperty(key) && key !== 'callback') {
          requestData[key] = e.parameter[key];
        }
      }
    }
    
    var action = requestData.action || "login";
    
    if (action === "login") {
      return handleLogin(requestData, e);
    } else if (action === "validateSession") {
      return handleValidateSession(requestData, e);
    } else if (action === "logout") {
      return handleLogout(requestData, e);
    } else if (action === "uploadLogo") {
      return handleUploadLogoPost(requestData, e);
    } else if (action === "createLogo") {
      return handleCreateLogoPost(requestData, e);
    } else if (action === "updateLogo") {
      return handleUpdateLogoPost(requestData, e);
    } else {
      return createJsonResponse({ 
        status: "error", 
        message: "Invalid action" 
      });
    }
  } catch (error) {
    return createJsonResponse({ 
      status: "error", 
      message: "Server error: " + error.toString()
    });
  }
}

// ============================================
// LOGIN HANDLER
// ============================================

function handleLogin(requestData, e) {
  var loginid = requestData.loginid;
  var password = requestData.password;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate input
  if (!loginid || !password) {
    logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, false, "Missing credentials");
    return createJsonResponse({ 
      status: "error", 
      message: "Missing credentials" 
    });
  }
  
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  
  // Check rate limiting
  var rateLimitResult = checkRateLimit(ss, ipAddress, loginid);
  if (!rateLimitResult.allowed) {
    logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, false, rateLimitResult.message);
    var result = { status: "error", message: rateLimitResult.message };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// OPENING STOCK BALANCE HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all opening stock records
  
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    if (loginData[i][0] == loginid && loginData[i][2] == password) {
      var roleId = loginData[i][3];
      var userName = loginData[i][1];
      
      // Get role name
      var rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Roles");
      var rolesData = rolesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var roleName = "";
      
      for (var j = 1; j < rolesData.length; j++) {
        if (rolesData[j][0] == roleId) {
          roleName = rolesData[j][1];
          break;
        }
      }
      
      // Get menu access
      var menuAccess = getRoleMenuAccess(ss, roleId);
      
      // Create session
      var sessionId = generateSessionId();
      var now = new Date().getTime();
      var expiryTime = now + SESSION_DURATION;
      
      // Store session in ActiveSessions tab
      var sessionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ActiveSessions");
      sessionsSheet.appendRow([
        sessionId,
        loginid,
        userName,
        roleId,
        new Date(now),
        new Date(expiryTime),
        ipAddress,
        new Date(now)
      ]);
      
      // Log successful login
      logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, true, "Login successful");
      
      // Return session data
      return createJsonResponse({ 
        status: "success", 
        sessionId: sessionId,
        userId: loginid,
        userName: userName,
        roleId: roleId,
        roleName: roleName,
        menuAccess: menuAccess,
        expiryTime: expiryTime
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Invalid credentials
  logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, false, "Invalid credentials");
  return createJsonResponse({ 
    status: "error", 
    message: "Invalid credentials" 
  });
}
function handleGetOpeningStocksGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all records from SOPHeader sheet
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", openingStocks: [], totalRecords: 0, totalPages: 0, currentPage: page, pageSize: pageSize };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allRecords = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var narration = headerData[i][4] || '';
      var date = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply search filter if search term is provided
      if (search) {
        if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue; // Skip this record if no match
        }
      }
      
      allRecords.push({
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: date,
        amount: headerData[i][3] || 0,
        narration: narration,
        createdBy: headerData[i][5] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][6] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][7] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][8] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo (most recent/last document first)
  allRecords.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allRecords.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  // Get only the records for the requested page
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var records = allRecords.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    openingStocks: records, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single opening stock by DocNo
function handleGetOpeningStockByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Opening stock record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: headerData[i][1],
        date: headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        amount: headerData[i][3] || 0,
        narration: headerData[i][4] || '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][5] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][6] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][7] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][8] || ''
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Opening stock record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet ? detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: detailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: detailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: detailsData[i][5] || 0,
        unit: detailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: detailsData[i][7] || 0,
        amount: detailsData[i][8] || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", openingStock: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new opening stock
function handleCreateOpeningStockGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var openingStockDataJson = e.parameter.openingStockData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse data
  var openingStockData;
  try {
    openingStockData = JSON.parse(openingStockDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!openingStockData.header || !openingStockData.details || openingStockData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    headerSheet = ss.insertSheet("SOPHeader");
  headerSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "Date", "Amount", "Narration", "CreatedBy", "CreatedTime", "PostedBy", "PostedTime"]);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000; // Starting number
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('SOP')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(3));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'SOP' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = openingStockData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  headerSheet.appendRow([
    'SOP', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.amount || 0,
    header.narration || '',
    validation.userName, // CreatedBy
    createdTime
  ]);
  
  // Add details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("SOPDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "StockCode", "StockDescription", "Qty", "Unit", "Rate", "Amount"]);
  }
  for (var i = 0; i < openingStockData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = openingStockData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'SOP', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.amount || 0
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Opening stock created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing opening stock
function handleUpdateOpeningStockGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var openingStockDataJson = e.parameter.openingStockData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse data
  var openingStockData;
  try {
    openingStockData = JSON.parse(openingStockDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!openingStockData.header || !openingStockData.header.docNo || !openingStockData.details || openingStockData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = openingStockData.header.docNo;
  
  // Update header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Opening stock record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      var header = openingStockData.header;
      headerSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 7).setValues([[
        'SOP', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.amount || 0,
        header.narration || '',
        headerData[i][5], // Keep original CreatedBy
        headerData[i][6]  // Keep original CreatedTime
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Opening stock record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  } else {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("SOPDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "StockCode", "StockDescription", "Qty", "Unit", "Rate", "Amount"]);
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < openingStockData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = openingStockData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'SOP', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.amount || 0
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Opening stock updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete opening stock
function handleDeleteOpeningStockGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Opening stock record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Opening stock record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Opening stock deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// GET version of login (JSONP)
function handleLoginGet(e) {
  var loginid = e.parameter.loginid;
  var password = e.parameter.password;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  if (!loginid || !password) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Missing credentials" };
    logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, false, "Missing credentials");
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  
  // Check rate limiting
  var rateLimitResult = checkRateLimit(ss, ipAddress, loginid);
  if (!rateLimitResult.allowed) {
    logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, false, rateLimitResult.message);
    var result = { status: "error", message: rateLimitResult.message };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  }
  
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var result = { status: "error", message: "Invalid credentials" };
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    if (loginData[i][0] == loginid && loginData[i][2] == password) {
      var roleId = loginData[i][3];
      var userName = loginData[i][1];
      
      var rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Roles");
      var rolesData = rolesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var roleName = "";
      
      for (var j = 1; j < rolesData.length; j++) {
        if (rolesData[j][0] == roleId) {
          roleName = rolesData[j][1];
          break;
        }
      }
      
      var menuAccess = getRoleMenuAccess(ss, roleId);
      var sessionId = generateSessionId();
      var now = new Date().getTime();
      var expiryTime = now + SESSION_DURATION;
      
      var sessionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ActiveSessions");
      sessionsSheet.appendRow([
        sessionId,
        loginid,
        userName,
        roleId,
        new Date(now),
        new Date(expiryTime),
        ipAddress,
        new Date(now)
      ]);
      
      logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, true, "Login successful");
      
      result = { 
        status: "success", 
        sessionId: sessionId,
        userId: loginid,
        userName: userName,
        roleId: roleId,
        roleName: roleName,
        menuAccess: menuAccess,
        expiryTime: expiryTime
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (result.status === "error") {
    logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, loginid, false, "Invalid credentials");
  }
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
// ============================================
// SESSION VALIDATION HANDLER
// ============================================

function handleValidateSession(requestData, e) {
  var sessionId = requestData.sessionId;
  var userId = requestData.userId;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  if (!sessionId || !userId) {
    return createJsonResponse({ 
      status: "error",
      valid: false,
      message: "Missing session data" 
    });
  }
  
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  return createJsonResponse(validation);
}

function handleValidateSessionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  if (!sessionId || !userId) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", valid: false, message: "Missing session data" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(validation) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(validation);
}

// ============================================
// LOGOUT HANDLER
// ============================================

function handleLogout(requestData, e) {
  var sessionId = requestData.sessionId;
  
  if (!sessionId) {
    return createJsonResponse({ 
      status: "error", 
      message: "Missing session ID" 
    });
  }
  
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sessionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ActiveSessions");
  var data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and delete session
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] == sessionId) {
      sessionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      return createJsonResponse({ 
        status: "success", 
        message: "Logged out successfully" 
      });
    }
  }
  
  return createJsonResponse({ 
    status: "success", 
    message: "Session not found or already expired" 
  });
}

function handleLogoutGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  
  if (!sessionId) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Missing session ID" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sessionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ActiveSessions");
  var data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] == sessionId) {
      sessionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      var result = { status: "success", message: "Logged out successfully" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(result);
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Session not found or already expired" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ============================================

// Validate session
function validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress) {
  var sessionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ActiveSessions");
  var data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date().getTime();
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] == sessionId && data[i][1] == userId) {
      var expiryTime = new Date(data[i][5]).getTime();
      var sessionIP = data[i][6];
      
      // Check if expired
      if (now > expiryTime) {
        sessionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        return { 
          status: "error",
          valid: false, 
          message: "Session expired" 
        };
      }
      
      // Check IP address match
      if (sessionIP !== ipAddress) {
        return { 
          status: "error",
          valid: false, 
          message: "IP address mismatch - security violation" 
        };
      }
      
      // Update last activity
      sessionsSheet.getRange(i + 1, 8).setValue(new Date());
      
      // Get user data
      var userName = data[i][2];
      var roleId = data[i][3];
      
      // Get role name
      var rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Roles");
      var rolesData = rolesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var roleName = "";
      
      for (var j = 1; j < rolesData.length; j++) {
        if (rolesData[j][0] == roleId) {
          roleName = rolesData[j][1];
          break;
        }
      }
      
      // Get menu access
      var menuAccess = getRoleMenuAccess(ss, roleId);
      
      return { 
        status: "success",
        valid: true,
        userId: userId,
        userName: userName,
        roleId: roleId,
        roleName: roleName,
        menuAccess: menuAccess,
        expiryTime: expiryTime
      };
    }
  }
  
  return { 
    status: "error",
    valid: false, 
    message: "Invalid session" 
  };
}

// Check rate limiting
function checkRateLimit(ss, ipAddress, userId) {
  var attemptsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LoginAttempts");
  var data = attemptsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date().getTime();
  var windowStart = now - ATTEMPT_WINDOW;
  
  var ipAttempts = 0;
  var userAttempts = 0;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var attemptTime = new Date(data[i][0]).getTime();
    if (attemptTime > windowStart) {
      var attemptIP = data[i][1];
      var attemptUser = data[i][2];
      var success = data[i][3];
      
      if (!success) {
        if (attemptIP === ipAddress) {
          ipAttempts++;
        }
        if (attemptUser === userId) {
          userAttempts++;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  if (ipAttempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS) {
    return { 
      allowed: false, 
      message: "Too many failed attempts from this IP. Try again in 15 minutes." 
    };
  }
  
  if (userAttempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS) {
    return { 
      allowed: false, 
      message: "Too many failed attempts for this user. Try again in 15 minutes." 
    };
  }
  
  return { allowed: true };
}

// Log login attempt
function logLoginAttempt(ipAddress, userId, success, message) {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    var attemptsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LoginAttempts");
    attemptsSheet.appendRow([
      new Date(),
      ipAddress,
      userId,
      success,
      message
    ]);
  } catch (error) {
    // Fail silently if logging fails
  }
}

// Generate secure session ID
function generateSessionId() {
  return 'sess_' + Utilities.getUuid();
}

// Get client IP address
function getClientIP(e) {
  try {
    // Try to get real IP from headers
    if (e.parameter && e.parameter['X-Forwarded-For']) {
      return e.parameter['X-Forwarded-For'].split(',')[0].trim();
    }
    // Fallback to a placeholder
    return 'unknown';
  } catch (error) {
    return 'unknown';
  }
}

// Get menu access for role
function getRoleMenuAccess(ss, roleId) {
  var roleMenuSheet = ss.getSheetByName("RoleMenuAccess");
  var roleMenuData = roleMenuSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  var menuMasterSheet = ss.getSheetByName("MenuMaster");
  var menuMasterData = menuMasterSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  var allowedMenuIds = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < roleMenuData.length; i++) {
    if (roleMenuData[i][0] == roleId) {
      allowedMenuIds.push(roleMenuData[i][1]);
    }
  }
  
  var menus = [];
  for (var j = 1; j < menuMasterData.length; j++) {
    var menuId = menuMasterData[j][0];
    
    if (allowedMenuIds.indexOf(menuId) !== -1) {
      menus.push({
        menuId: menuId,
        parentMenuId: menuMasterData[j][1],
        menuType: menuMasterData[j][2],
        menuName: menuMasterData[j][3],
        pageUrl: menuMasterData[j][4],
        sortOrder: menuMasterData[j][5]
      });
    }
  }
  
  menus.sort(function(a, b) {
    return a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder;
  });
  
  return menus;
}

// Create JSON response
function createJsonResponse(data) {
  var output = ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(data))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
  return output;
}

// ============================================
// USER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS (LOGIN MASTER)
// ============================================

// Get all users
function handleGetUsersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all users from LogIn sheet
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var users = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    users.push({
      loginId: loginData[i][0],
      userName: loginData[i][1],
      password: loginData[i][2],
      roleId: loginData[i][3]
    });
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", users: users };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get all roles

  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;

  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);

    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };

      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);

    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
