// Create new user
function handleCreateUserGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var loginId = e.parameter.loginId;
  var userName = e.parameter.userName;
  var password = e.parameter.password;
  var roleId = e.parameter.roleId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!loginId || !userName || !password || !roleId) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "All fields are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if loginId already exists
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    if (loginData[i][0] == loginId) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Login ID already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new user
  loginSheet.appendRow([loginId, userName, password, roleId]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "User created successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing user
function handleUpdateUserGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var loginId = e.parameter.loginId;
  var userName = e.parameter.userName;
  var password = e.parameter.password;
  var roleId = e.parameter.roleId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!loginId || !userName || !password || !roleId) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "All fields are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Find and update user
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var found = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    if (loginData[i][0] == loginId) {
      loginSheet.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(userName);
      loginSheet.getRange(i + 1, 3).setValue(password);
      loginSheet.getRange(i + 1, 4).setValue(roleId);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "User not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "User updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete user
function handleDeleteUserGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var loginId = e.parameter.loginId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Prevent deleting own account
  if (loginId == userId) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot delete your own account" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Find and delete user
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var found = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    if (loginData[i][0] == loginId) {
      loginSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "User not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "User deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// ROLE MANAGEMENT HANDLERS (ROLE MASTER)
// ============================================

// Create new role
function handleCreateRoleGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var rolesName = e.parameter.rolesName;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!rolesName) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Role name is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Roles");
  var rolesData = rolesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if role name already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < rolesData.length; i++) {
    if (rolesData[i][1] == rolesName) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Role name already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Find max RolesId and increment
  var maxId = 0;
  for (var i = 1; i < rolesData.length; i++) {
    if (rolesData[i][0] > maxId) {
      maxId = rolesData[i][0];
    }
  }
  var newRolesId = maxId + 1;
  
  // Add new role
  rolesSheet.appendRow([newRolesId, rolesName]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Role created successfully", rolesId: newRolesId };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing role
function handleUpdateRoleGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var rolesId = e.parameter.rolesId;
  var rolesName = e.parameter.rolesName;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!rolesId || !rolesName) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Role ID and name are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Roles");
  var rolesData = rolesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if new role name already exists (excluding current role)
  for (var i = 1; i < rolesData.length; i++) {
    if (rolesData[i][1] == rolesName && rolesData[i][0] != rolesId) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Role name already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Find and update role
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < rolesData.length; i++) {
    if (rolesData[i][0] == rolesId) {
      rolesSheet.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(rolesName);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Role not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Role updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get role usage statistics
function handleGetRoleUsageGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all roles
  var rolesSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Roles");
  var rolesData = rolesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Get all users
  var loginSheet = ss.getSheetByName("LogIn");
  var loginData = loginSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Count users per role
  var usage = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < rolesData.length; i++) {
    var roleId = rolesData[i][0];
    usage[roleId] = 0;
  }
  
  for (var i = 1; i < loginData.length; i++) {
    var roleId = loginData[i][3];
    if (usage.hasOwnProperty(roleId)) {
      usage[roleId]++;
    } else {
      usage[roleId] = 1;
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", usage: usage };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// MENU ACCESS MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all menus from MenuMaster
function handleGetAllMenusGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all menus from MenuMaster sheet
  var menuMasterSheet = ss.getSheetByName("MenuMaster");
  var menuMasterData = menuMasterSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var menus = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < menuMasterData.length; i++) {
    menus.push({
      menuId: menuMasterData[i][0],
      parentMenuId: menuMasterData[i][1],
      menuType: menuMasterData[i][2],
      menuName: menuMasterData[i][3],
      pageUrl: menuMasterData[i][4],
      sortOrder: menuMasterData[i][5]
    });
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", menus: menus };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get role menu access
function handleGetRoleMenuAccessGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var roleId = e.parameter.roleId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get menu IDs for this role from RoleMenuAccess sheet
  var roleMenuSheet = ss.getSheetByName("RoleMenuAccess");
  var roleMenuData = roleMenuSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var menuIds = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < roleMenuData.length; i++) {
    if (roleMenuData[i][0] == roleId) {
      menuIds.push(roleMenuData[i][1]);
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", menuIds: menuIds };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update role menu access
function handleUpdateRoleMenuAccessGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var roleId = e.parameter.roleId;
  var menuIdsJson = e.parameter.menuIds;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse menu IDs
  var menuIds = [];
  try {
    menuIds = JSON.parse(menuIdsJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid menu IDs format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var roleMenuSheet = ss.getSheetByName("RoleMenuAccess");
  var roleMenuData = roleMenuSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Delete existing assignments for this role (from bottom to top)
  for (var i = roleMenuData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (roleMenuData[i][0] == roleId) {
      roleMenuSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new assignments
  for (var j = 0; j < menuIds.length; j++) {
    roleMenuSheet.appendRow([roleId, menuIds[j]]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Menu access updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// MENU MASTER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Create new menu
function handleCreateMenuGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var menuName = e.parameter.menuName;
  var menuType = e.parameter.menuType;
  var parentMenuId = e.parameter.parentMenuId;
  var pageUrl = e.parameter.pageUrl;
  var sortOrder = e.parameter.sortOrder;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!menuName || !menuType || !sortOrder) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Menu name, type, and sort order are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var menuMasterSheet = ss.getSheetByName("MenuMaster");
  var menuMasterData = menuMasterSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find max MenuId and increment
  var maxId = 0;
  for (var i = 1; i < menuMasterData.length; i++) {
    if (menuMasterData[i][0] > maxId) {
      maxId = menuMasterData[i][0];
    }
  }
  var newMenuId = maxId + 1;
  
  // Add new menu
  menuMasterSheet.appendRow([
    newMenuId,
    parentMenuId === 'NULL' ? '' : parentMenuId,
    menuType,
    menuName,
    pageUrl === 'NULL' ? '' : pageUrl,
    sortOrder
  ]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Menu created successfully", menuId: newMenuId };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing menu
function handleUpdateMenuGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var menuId = e.parameter.menuId;
  var menuName = e.parameter.menuName;
  var menuType = e.parameter.menuType;
  var parentMenuId = e.parameter.parentMenuId;
  var pageUrl = e.parameter.pageUrl;
  var sortOrder = e.parameter.sortOrder;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!menuId || !menuName || !menuType || !sortOrder) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "All fields are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var menuMasterSheet = ss.getSheetByName("MenuMaster");
  var menuMasterData = menuMasterSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update menu
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < menuMasterData.length; i++) {
    if (menuMasterData[i][0] == menuId) {
      menuMasterSheet.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(parentMenuId === 'NULL' ? '' : parentMenuId);
      menuMasterSheet.getRange(i + 1, 3).setValue(menuType);
      menuMasterSheet.getRange(i + 1, 4).setValue(menuName);
      menuMasterSheet.getRange(i + 1, 5).setValue(pageUrl === 'NULL' ? '' : pageUrl);
      menuMasterSheet.getRange(i + 1, 6).setValue(sortOrder);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Menu not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Menu updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete menu

// ============================================
// SYSTEM PARAMETERS MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all system parameters
function handleGetSystemParametersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // All authenticated users can read system parameters (no admin check needed)
  // This allows CompanyName to be displayed for all users
  // Write operations (create/update/delete) still require Admin role
  
  // Get all parameters from SystemParameters sheet
  var paramsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SystemParameters");
  var paramsData = paramsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var parameters = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < paramsData.length; i++) {
    parameters.push({
      code: paramsData[i][0],
      value: paramsData[i][1],
      description: paramsData[i][2]
    });
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", parameters: parameters };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new system parameter
function handleCreateSystemParameterGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var value = e.parameter.value;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !value || !description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "All fields are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var paramsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SystemParameters");
  var paramsData = paramsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < paramsData.length; i++) {
    if (paramsData[i][0] == code) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Parameter code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new parameter
  paramsSheet.appendRow([code, value, description]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Parameter created successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing system parameter
function handleUpdateSystemParameterGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var value = e.parameter.value;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);

    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if user is admin
  if (validation.roleName !== "Admin") {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Access denied: Admin role required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !value || !description) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "All fields are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var paramsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SystemParameters");
  var paramsData = paramsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update parameter
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < paramsData.length; i++) {
    if (paramsData[i][0] == code) {
      paramsSheet.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(value);
      paramsSheet.getRange(i + 1, 3).setValue(description);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Parameter not found" };

    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get all units
function handleGetUnitsGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all units from UnitMaster sheet
  var unitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("UnitMaster");
  var unitData = unitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var units = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < unitData.length; i++) {
    units.push({
      unitCode: unitData[i][0],
      unitDescription: unitData[i][1]
    });
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", units: units };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }

// Create new unit
function handleCreateUnitGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var unitCode = e.parameter.unitCode;
  var unitDescription = e.parameter.unitDescription;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!unitCode || !unitDescription) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Unit code and description are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var unitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("UnitMaster");
  var unitData = unitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if unit code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < unitData.length; i++) {
    if (unitData[i][0] == unitCode) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Unit code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new unit
  unitSheet.appendRow([unitCode, unitDescription]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Unit created successfully", unitCode: unitCode };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var unitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("UnitMaster");
  var unitData = unitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find the unit first
  var found = false;
  var unitRowIndex = -1;
  for (var i = 1; i < unitData.length; i++) {
    if (unitData[i][0] && unitData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == unitCode.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      unitRowIndex = i + 1;
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Unit not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check for references before deleting.
  // If references exist, block the delete completely to avoid orphaned data.
  var refs = countUnitReferences(ss, unitCode);
  var refParts = [];
  if (refs.stockMaster > 0) refParts.push(refs.stockMaster + " stock item(s)");
  if (refs.stockUnits > 0) refParts.push(refs.stockUnits + " stock unit conversion(s)");
  if (refs.stockTransactions > 0) refParts.push(refs.stockTransactions + " stock transaction(s)");
  if (refs.openingStockDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.openingStockDetails + " opening stock detail(s)");
  if (refs.salesDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.salesDetails + " sales detail(s)");
  if (refs.purchaseDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.purchaseDetails + " purchase detail(s)");
  if (refs.adjDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.adjDetails + " adjustment detail(s)");
  
  if (refParts.length > 0) {
    var msg = "Cannot delete: Unit '" + unitCode + "' is in use by " + refParts.join(", ") + ". Delete or unlink these records first.";
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: msg };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // No references found - delete the unit from UnitMaster
  unitSheet.deleteRow(unitRowIndex);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Unit deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Count references to a unit code across stock master, stock unit conversions,
// posted stock transactions, and transaction detail sheets
function countUnitReferences(ss, unitCode) {
  var refs = {
    stockMaster: 0,
    stockUnits: 0,
    stockTransactions: 0,
    openingStockDetails: 0,
    salesDetails: 0,
    purchaseDetails: 0,
    adjDetails: 0
  };
  
  if (!unitCode) return refs;
  
  var unitUpper = unitCode.toString().toUpperCase();
  
  // StockMaster sheet - unit code at index 2 (base unit)
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  if (stockSheet) {
    var stockData = stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
      if (stockData[i][2] && stockData[i][2].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.stockMaster++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // StockUnit sheet - unit at index 1 (conversion units)
  var stockUnitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockUnit");
  if (stockUnitSheet) {
    var stockUnitData = stockUnitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < stockUnitData.length; i++) {
      if (stockUnitData[i][1] && stockUnitData[i][1].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.stockUnits++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // StockTransaction sheet - BaseUnit at index 10
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (stockTransactionSheet) {
    var txData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < txData.length; i++) {
      if (txData[i][10] && txData[i][10].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.stockTransactions++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // SOPDetails sheet - unit at index 6
  var sopDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  if (sopDetailsSheet) {
    var sopData = sopDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < sopData.length; i++) {
      if (sopData[i][6] && sopData[i][6].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.openingStockDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // SalesDetails sheet - unit at index 6
  var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  if (salesDetailsSheet) {
    var salesData = salesDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < salesData.length; i++) {
      if (salesData[i][6] && salesData[i][6].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.salesDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // PurchaseDetails sheet - unit at index 6
  var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  if (purchaseDetailsSheet) {
    var purchaseData = purchaseDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < purchaseData.length; i++) {
      if (purchaseData[i][6] && purchaseData[i][6].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.purchaseDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // ADJDetails sheet - unit at index 6
  var adjDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  if (adjDetailsSheet) {
    var adjData = adjDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < adjData.length; i++) {
      if (adjData[i][6] && adjData[i][6].toString().toUpperCase() == unitUpper) {
        refs.adjDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return refs;
}

// ============================================
// STOCK MASTER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all stocks
function handleGetStocksGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all stocks from StockMaster sheet
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var stocks = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      stocks.push({
        code: stockData[i][0],
        description: stockData[i][1] || '',
        unitCode: stockData[i][2] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", stocks: stocks };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new stock
function handleCreateStockGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var unitCode = e.parameter.unitCode;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description || !unitCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Code, Description, and UnitCode are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Check if stock code already exists
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0] && stockData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock code already exists" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Validate UnitCode exists in UnitMaster
  var unitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("UnitMaster");
  var unitData = unitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var unitExists = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < unitData.length; i++) {
    if (unitData[i][0] && unitData[i][0].toString() == unitCode.toString()) {
      unitExists = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!unitExists) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid Unit Code. Unit does not exist in Unit Master." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Add new stock
  stockSheet.appendRow([code, description, unitCode]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock created successfully", code: code };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing stock
function handleUpdateStockGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description;
  var unitCode = e.parameter.unitCode;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code || !description || !unitCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Code, Description, and UnitCode are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate UnitCode exists in UnitMaster
  var unitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("UnitMaster");
  var unitData = unitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var unitExists = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < unitData.length; i++) {
    if (unitData[i][0] && unitData[i][0].toString() == unitCode.toString()) {
      unitExists = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!unitExists) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid Unit Code. Unit does not exist in Unit Master." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues();

  // Find and update stock
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0] && stockData[i][0].toString() == code.toString()) {
      // Base unit cannot be changed for an existing stock item - protect conversion integrity
      var existingUnitCode = stockData[i][2] ? stockData[i][2].toString() : '';
      if (existingUnitCode != unitCode.toString()) {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Base unit cannot be changed for an existing stock item." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      stockSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[code, description, unitCode]]);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete stock
function handleDeleteStockGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find the stock first
  var found = false;
  var stockRowIndex = -1;
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0] && stockData[i][0].toString() == code.toString()) {
      stockRowIndex = i + 1;
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check for transaction/history references before deleting.
  // If references exist, block the delete completely - do NOT delete units or the stock.
  var refs = countStockReferences(ss, code);
  var refParts = [];
  if (refs.stockTransactions > 0) refParts.push(refs.stockTransactions + " stock transaction(s)");
  if (refs.openingStockDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.openingStockDetails + " opening stock detail(s)");
  if (refs.salesDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.salesDetails + " sales detail(s)");
  if (refs.purchaseDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.purchaseDetails + " purchase detail(s)");
  if (refs.adjDetails > 0) refParts.push(refs.adjDetails + " adjustment detail(s)");
  
  if (refParts.length > 0) {
    var msg = "Cannot delete: Stock '" + code + "' is referenced in " + refParts.join(", ") + ". Delete or unlink these records first.";
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: msg };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // No references found - cascade delete stock units for this stock code
  var stockUnitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockUnit");
  if (stockUnitSheet) {
    var unitData = stockUnitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var u = unitData.length - 1; u >= 1; u--) {
      if (unitData[u][0] && unitData[u][0].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
        stockUnitSheet.deleteRow(u + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Delete the stock from StockMaster
  stockSheet.deleteRow(stockRowIndex);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Count references to a stock code across transaction/history sheets
function countStockReferences(ss, code) {
  var refs = {
    stockTransactions: 0,
    openingStockDetails: 0,
    salesDetails: 0,
    purchaseDetails: 0,
    adjDetails: 0
  };
  
  if (!code) return refs;
  
  // StockTransaction sheet - stock code at index 7
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (stockTransactionSheet) {
    var txData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < txData.length; i++) {
      if (txData[i][7] && txData[i][7].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
        refs.stockTransactions++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // SOPDetails sheet - stock code at index 3
  var sopDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  if (sopDetailsSheet) {
    var sopData = sopDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < sopData.length; i++) {
      if (sopData[i][3] && sopData[i][3].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
        refs.openingStockDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // SalesDetails sheet - stock code at index 3
  var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  if (salesDetailsSheet) {
    var salesData = salesDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < salesData.length; i++) {
      if (salesData[i][3] && salesData[i][3].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
        refs.salesDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // PurchaseDetails sheet - stock code at index 3
  var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  if (purchaseDetailsSheet) {
    var purchaseData = purchaseDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < purchaseData.length; i++) {
      if (purchaseData[i][3] && purchaseData[i][3].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
        refs.purchaseDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // ADJDetails sheet - stock code at index 3
  var adjDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  if (adjDetailsSheet) {
    var adjData = adjDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < adjData.length; i++) {
      if (adjData[i][3] && adjData[i][3].toString().toUpperCase() == code.toString().toUpperCase()) {
        refs.adjDetails++;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return refs;
}

// ============================================
// STOCK UNIT MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all stock units for a specific stock code
function handleGetStockUnitsGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var stockCode = e.parameter.stockCode;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!stockCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock code is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get stock units from StockUnit sheet
  var stockUnitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockUnit");
  if (!stockUnitSheet) {
    // Create the sheet if it doesn't exist
    stockUnitSheet = ss.insertSheet("StockUnit");
    stockUnitSheet.appendRow(["StockCode", "Unit", "FromQty", "ToQty"]);
  }
  
  var stockUnitData = stockUnitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var stockUnits = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < stockUnitData.length; i++) {
    if (stockUnitData[i][0] && stockUnitData[i][0].toString() == stockCode.toString()) {
      stockUnits.push({
        stockCode: stockUnitData[i][0].toString(),
        unit: stockUnitData[i][1].toString(),
        fromQty: parseFloat(stockUnitData[i][2]) || 0,
        toQty: parseFloat(stockUnitData[i][3]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", stockUnits: stockUnits };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new stock unit
function handleCreateStockUnitGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var stockCode = e.parameter.stockCode;
  var unit = e.parameter.unit;
  var fromQty = e.parameter.fromQty;
  var toQty = e.parameter.toQty;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!stockCode || !unit || !fromQty || !toQty) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "StockCode, Unit, FromQty, and ToQty are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate stock code exists
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var stockExists = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0] && stockData[i][0].toString() == stockCode.toString()) {
      stockExists = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!stockExists) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock code does not exist" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get or create StockUnit sheet
  var stockUnitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockUnit");
  if (!stockUnitSheet) {
    stockUnitSheet = ss.insertSheet("StockUnit");
    stockUnitSheet.appendRow(["StockCode", "Unit", "FromQty", "ToQty"]);
  }
  
  // Check for duplicate unit for this stock code
  var stockUnitData = stockUnitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < stockUnitData.length; i++) {
    if (stockUnitData[i][0] && stockUnitData[i][0].toString() == stockCode.toString() &&
        stockUnitData[i][1] && stockUnitData[i][1].toString() == unit.toString()) {
      var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "This unit is already defined for this stock item" };
      if (callback) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
      }
      return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
    }
  }
  
  // Add new stock unit
  stockUnitSheet.appendRow([stockCode, unit, parseFloat(fromQty), parseFloat(toQty)]);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock unit created successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing stock unit
function handleUpdateStockUnitGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var stockCode = e.parameter.stockCode;
  var unit = e.parameter.unit;
  var fromQty = e.parameter.fromQty;
  var toQty = e.parameter.toQty;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!stockCode || !unit || !fromQty || !toQty) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "StockCode, Unit, FromQty, and ToQty are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockUnitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockUnit");
  if (!stockUnitSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No stock units found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockUnitData = stockUnitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update stock unit
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < stockUnitData.length; i++) {
    if (stockUnitData[i][0] && stockUnitData[i][0].toString() == stockCode.toString() &&
        stockUnitData[i][1] && stockUnitData[i][1].toString() == unit.toString()) {
      stockUnitSheet.getRange(i + 1, 3).setValue(parseFloat(fromQty));
      stockUnitSheet.getRange(i + 1, 4).setValue(parseFloat(toQty));
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock unit not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock unit updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

function handleDeleteStockUnitGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var stockCode = e.parameter.stockCode;

  var callback = e.parameter.callback;

      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);

    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "StockCode and Unit are required" };

    }

  for (var i = 1; i < stockUnitData.length; i++) {
    if (stockUnitData[i][0] && stockUnitData[i][0].toString() == stockCode.toString() &&
        stockUnitData[i][1] && stockUnitData[i][1].toString() == unit.toString()) {
      stockUnitSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      found = true;
      break;

    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock unit not found" };
