// ============================================
// SALES REPORT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get sales report with date range and filters
function handleGetSalesReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read filter parameters
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var customerFilter = (e.parameter.customer || '').toLowerCase();
  var salesmanFilter = (e.parameter.salesman || '').toLowerCase();
  var reportType = (e.parameter.reportType || 'headerwise').toLowerCase();
  
  // Build customer lookup map (code -> description)
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var customerMap = {};
  for (var c = 1; c < customerData.length; c++) {
    if (customerData[c][0]) {
      customerMap[String(customerData[c][0]).toLowerCase()] = customerData[c][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Build salesman lookup map (code -> description)
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var salesmanMap = {};
  for (var s = 1; s < salesmanData.length; s++) {
    if (salesmanData[s][0]) {
      salesmanMap[String(salesmanData[s][0]).toLowerCase()] = salesmanData[s][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Get all sales from SalesHeader sheet
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allSales = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = salesHeaderData[i][1].toString();
      var customer = salesHeaderData[i][3] || '';
      var salesman = String(salesHeaderData[i][18] || '');
      var dateObj = salesHeaderData[i][2] ? new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply date range filter
      if (fromDate && dateStr < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      // Apply customer filter
      if (customerFilter && customer.toLowerCase().indexOf(customerFilter) === -1) continue;
      
      // Apply salesman filter
      if (salesmanFilter && salesman.toLowerCase().indexOf(salesmanFilter) === -1) continue;
      
      // Look up customer and salesman names
      var customerName = customerMap[customer.toLowerCase()] || '';
      var salesmanName = salesmanMap[salesman.toLowerCase()] || '';
      
      allSales.push({
        docType: salesHeaderData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: dateStr,
        customer: customer,
        customerName: customerName,
        salesman: salesman,
        salesmanName: salesmanName,
        narration: salesHeaderData[i][4] || '',
        grossAmount: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][5]) || 0,
        discountPercentage: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][6]) || 0,
        discountAmount: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][7]) || 0,
        vat: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][8]) || 0,
        net: parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][9]) || 0,
        vatTreatment: salesHeaderData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: salesHeaderData[i][15] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date descending, then by DocNo descending
  allSales.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return b.date.localeCompare(a.date);
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  // If reportType is itemwise, fetch item details for each sale
  if (reportType === 'itemwise') {
    var salesDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
    var salesDetailsData = salesDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    // Build a map of docNo -> details array
    var detailsMap = {};
    for (var d = 1; d < salesDetailsData.length; d++) {
      var detailDocNo = salesDetailsData[d][1];
      if (detailDocNo) {
        if (!detailsMap[detailDocNo]) {
          detailsMap[detailDocNo] = [];
        }
        detailsMap[detailDocNo].push({
          docSrNo: salesDetailsData[d][2] || 0,
          stockCode: salesDetailsData[d][3] || '',
          stockDescription: salesDetailsData[d][4] || '',
          qty: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][5]) || 0,
          unit: salesDetailsData[d][6] || '',
          rate: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][7]) || 0,
          grossAmount: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][8]) || 0,
          discountAmount: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][9]) || 0,
          discountPercentage: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][10]) || 0,
          vatAmount: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][11]) || 0,
          vatPercentage: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][12]) || 0,
          netAmount: parseFloat(salesDetailsData[d][13]) || 0,
          vatTreatment: salesDetailsData[d][14] || '',
          rateType: salesDetailsData[d][15] || ''
        });
      }
    }
    
    // Attach details to each sale
    for (var si = 0; si < allSales.length; si++) {
      allSales[si].details = detailsMap[allSales[si].docNo] || [];
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary
  var totalCount = allSales.length;
  var totalGross = 0;
  var totalDiscount = 0;
  var totalVat = 0;
  var totalNet = 0;
  var totalItemQty = 0;
  
  for (var i = 0; i < allSales.length; i++) {
    totalGross += allSales[i].grossAmount;
    totalDiscount += allSales[i].discountAmount;
    totalVat += allSales[i].vat;
    totalNet += allSales[i].net;
    if (reportType === 'itemwise' && allSales[i].details) {
      for (var di = 0; di < allSales[i].details.length; di++) {
        totalItemQty += allSales[i].details[di].qty;
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    sales: allSales,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: totalCount,
      totalGross: Math.round(totalGross * 100) / 100,
      totalDiscount: Math.round(totalDiscount * 100) / 100,
      totalVat: Math.round(totalVat * 100) / 100,
      totalNet: Math.round(totalNet * 100) / 100,
      totalItemQty: Math.round(totalItemQty * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// PURCHASE REPORT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get purchase report with date range and filters
function handleGetPurchaseReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read filter parameters
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var supplierFilter = (e.parameter.supplier || '').toLowerCase();
  var salesmanFilter = (e.parameter.salesman || '').toLowerCase();
  var reportType = (e.parameter.reportType || 'headerwise').toLowerCase();
  
  // Build supplier lookup map (code -> description)
  var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
  var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var supplierMap = {};
  for (var c = 1; c < supplierData.length; c++) {
    if (supplierData[c][0]) {
      supplierMap[String(supplierData[c][0]).toLowerCase()] = supplierData[c][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Build salesman lookup map (code -> description)
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var salesmanMap = {};
  for (var s = 1; s < salesmanData.length; s++) {
    if (salesmanData[s][0]) {
      salesmanMap[String(salesmanData[s][0]).toLowerCase()] = salesmanData[s][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Get all purchases from PurchaseHeader sheet
  var purchaseHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  var purchaseHeaderData = purchaseHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allPurchases = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < purchaseHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (purchaseHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = purchaseHeaderData[i][1].toString();
      var supplier = purchaseHeaderData[i][3] || '';
      var salesman = String(purchaseHeaderData[i][18] || '');
      var dateObj = purchaseHeaderData[i][2] ? new Date(purchaseHeaderData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply date range filter
      if (fromDate && dateStr < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      // Apply supplier filter
      if (supplierFilter && supplier.toLowerCase().indexOf(supplierFilter) === -1) continue;
      
      // Apply salesman filter
      if (salesmanFilter && salesman.toLowerCase().indexOf(salesmanFilter) === -1) continue;
      
      // Look up supplier and salesman names
      var supplierName = supplierMap[supplier.toLowerCase()] || '';
      var salesmanName = salesmanMap[salesman.toLowerCase()] || '';
      
      allPurchases.push({
        docType: purchaseHeaderData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: dateStr,
        supplier: supplier,
        supplierName: supplierName,
        salesman: salesman,
        salesmanName: salesmanName,
        narration: purchaseHeaderData[i][4] || '',
        grossAmount: parseFloat(purchaseHeaderData[i][5]) || 0,
        discountPercentage: parseFloat(purchaseHeaderData[i][6]) || 0,
        discountAmount: parseFloat(purchaseHeaderData[i][7]) || 0,
        vat: parseFloat(purchaseHeaderData[i][8]) || 0,
        net: parseFloat(purchaseHeaderData[i][9]) || 0,
        vatTreatment: purchaseHeaderData[i][14] || '',
        rateType: purchaseHeaderData[i][15] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date descending, then by DocNo descending
  allPurchases.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return b.date.localeCompare(a.date);
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  // If reportType is itemwise, fetch item details for each purchase
  if (reportType === 'itemwise') {
    var purchaseDetailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
    var purchaseDetailsData = purchaseDetailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    // Build a map of docNo -> details array
    var detailsMap = {};
    for (var d = 1; d < purchaseDetailsData.length; d++) {
      var detailDocNo = purchaseDetailsData[d][1];
      if (detailDocNo) {
        if (!detailsMap[detailDocNo]) {
          detailsMap[detailDocNo] = [];
        }
        detailsMap[detailDocNo].push({
          docSrNo: purchaseDetailsData[d][2] || 0,
          stockCode: purchaseDetailsData[d][3] || '',
          stockDescription: purchaseDetailsData[d][4] || '',
          qty: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][5]) || 0,
          unit: purchaseDetailsData[d][6] || '',
          rate: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][7]) || 0,
          grossAmount: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][8]) || 0,
          discountAmount: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][9]) || 0,
          discountPercentage: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][10]) || 0,
          vatAmount: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][11]) || 0,
          vatPercentage: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][12]) || 0,
          netAmount: parseFloat(purchaseDetailsData[d][13]) || 0,
          vatTreatment: purchaseDetailsData[d][14] || '',
          rateType: purchaseDetailsData[d][15] || ''
        });
      }
    }
    
    // Attach details to each purchase
    for (var pi = 0; pi < allPurchases.length; pi++) {
      allPurchases[pi].details = detailsMap[allPurchases[pi].docNo] || [];
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary
  var totalCount = allPurchases.length;
  var totalGross = 0;
  var totalDiscount = 0;
  var totalVat = 0;
  var totalNet = 0;
  var totalItemQty = 0;
  
  for (var i = 0; i < allPurchases.length; i++) {
    totalGross += allPurchases[i].grossAmount;
    totalDiscount += allPurchases[i].discountAmount;
    totalVat += allPurchases[i].vat;
    totalNet += allPurchases[i].net;
    if (reportType === 'itemwise' && allPurchases[i].details) {
      for (var di = 0; di < allPurchases[i].details.length; di++) {
        totalItemQty += allPurchases[i].details[di].qty;
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    purchases: allPurchases,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: totalCount,
      totalGross: Math.round(totalGross * 100) / 100,
      totalDiscount: Math.round(totalDiscount * 100) / 100,
      totalVat: Math.round(totalVat * 100) / 100,
      totalNet: Math.round(totalNet * 100) / 100,
      totalItemQty: Math.round(totalItemQty * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// OPENING STOCK REPORT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get opening stock report with date range and filters
function handleGetOpeningStockReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read filter parameters
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var reportType = (e.parameter.reportType || 'headerwise').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all opening stock records from SOPHeader sheet
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", openingStocks: [], summary: { totalCount: 0, totalQty: 0, totalAmount: 0 } };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allRecords = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var dateObj = headerData[i][2] ? new Date(headerData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply date range filter
      if (fromDate && dateStr < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      allRecords.push({
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: dateStr,
        amount: parseFloat(headerData[i][3]) || 0,
        narration: headerData[i][4] || '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][5] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][6] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][7] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][8] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date descending, then by DocNo descending
  allRecords.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return b.date.localeCompare(a.date);
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  // If reportType is itemwise, fetch item details for each opening stock
  if (reportType === 'itemwise') {
    var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
    if (detailsSheet) {
      var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      
      // Build a map of docNo -> details array
      var detailsMap = {};
      for (var d = 1; d < detailsData.length; d++) {
        var detailDocNo = detailsData[d][1];
        if (detailDocNo) {
          if (!detailsMap[detailDocNo]) {
            detailsMap[detailDocNo] = [];
          }
          detailsMap[detailDocNo].push({
            docSrNo: detailsData[d][2] || 0,
            stockCode: detailsData[d][3] || '',
            stockDescription: detailsData[d][4] || '',
            qty: parseFloat(detailsData[d][5]) || 0,
            unit: detailsData[d][6] || '',
            rate: parseFloat(detailsData[d][7]) || 0,
            amount: parseFloat(detailsData[d][8]) || 0
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Attach details to each record
      for (var pi = 0; pi < allRecords.length; pi++) {
        allRecords[pi].details = detailsMap[allRecords[pi].docNo] || [];
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary
  var totalCount = allRecords.length;
  var totalAmount = 0;
  var totalQty = 0;
  
  for (var i = 0; i < allRecords.length; i++) {
    totalAmount += allRecords[i].amount;
    if (reportType === 'itemwise' && allRecords[i].details) {
      for (var di = 0; di < allRecords[i].details.length; di++) {
        totalQty += allRecords[i].details[di].qty;
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    openingStocks: allRecords,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: totalCount,
      totalAmount: Math.round(totalAmount * 100) / 100,
      totalQty: Math.round(totalQty * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get stock adjustment report with date range and filters
function handleGetStockAdjustmentReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);

  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';

      for (var pi = 0; pi < allRecords.length; pi++) {
        allRecords[pi].details = detailsMap[allRecords[pi].docNo] || [];
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Calculate summary
  var totalCount = allRecords.length;
  var totalAmount = 0;
  var totalQty = 0;
  
  for (var i = 0; i < allRecords.length; i++) {
    totalAmount += allRecords[i].amount;

      for (var di = 0; di < allRecords[i].details.length; di++) {

  }
  
  var result = {

      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// CLEANUP FUNCTION (Run periodically)
// ============================================

function cleanupExpiredSessions() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var sessionsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ActiveSessions");
  var data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date().getTime();
  
  // Delete from bottom to top to avoid index issues
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    var expiryTime = new Date(data[i][5]).getTime();
    if (now > expiryTime) {
      sessionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
    }
  }
  
  Logger.log("Cleaned up expired sessions");
}

// ============================================
// DASHBOARD MASTER HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all dashboard records
function handleGetDashboardGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all records from Dashboard sheet
  var dashboardSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Dashboard");
  if (!dashboardSheet) {
    // Create sheet if it doesn't exist
    dashboardSheet = ss.insertSheet("Dashboard");
    dashboardSheet.appendRow(["Code", "Description", "Roles", "Users"]);
  }
  
  var dashboardData = dashboardSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var dashboard = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < dashboardData.length; i++) {
    if (dashboardData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      dashboard.push({
        code: dashboardData[i][0].toString(),
        description: dashboardData[i][1] || '',
        roles: dashboardData[i][2] || '',
        users: dashboardData[i][3] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", dashboard: dashboard };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get user-specific dashboard records (filtered by role and user)
function handleGetUserDashboardsGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var roleId = e.parameter.roleId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get all records from Dashboard sheet
  var dashboardSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Dashboard");
  if (!dashboardSheet) {
    var result = { status: "success", dashboard: [] };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  }
  
  var dashboardData = dashboardSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var userDashboard = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < dashboardData.length; i++) {
    if (dashboardData[i][0]) { // Check if Code exists
      var rolesStr = dashboardData[i][2] || '';
      var usersStr = dashboardData[i][3] || '';
      
      // Parse roles XML
      var roleMatch = false;
      if (rolesStr) {
        var roleRegex = new RegExp('<role>([^<]*)<\\/role>', 'gi');
        var roleMatchResult;
        while ((roleMatchResult = roleRegex.exec(rolesStr)) !== null) {
          if (roleMatchResult[1].trim() == roleId) {
            roleMatch = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      
      // Parse users XML
      var userMatch = false;
      if (usersStr) {
        var userRegex = new RegExp('<user>([^<]*)<\\/user>', 'gi');
        var userMatchResult;
        while ((userMatchResult = userRegex.exec(usersStr)) !== null) {
          if (userMatchResult[1].trim().toLowerCase() == userId.toLowerCase()) {
            userMatch = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      
      // If both roles and users are empty, show to everyone
      // Otherwise, check if user's role or user ID matches
      var showDashboard = false;
      if (!rolesStr && !usersStr) {
        showDashboard = true; // Visible to all
      } else if (roleMatch || userMatch) {
        showDashboard = true;
      }
      
      if (showDashboard) {
        userDashboard.push({
          code: dashboardData[i][0].toString(),
          description: dashboardData[i][1] || ''
        });
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", dashboard: userDashboard };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update dashboard record
function handleUpdateDashboardGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var code = e.parameter.code;
  var description = e.parameter.description || '';
  var roles = e.parameter.roles || '';
  var users = e.parameter.users || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!code) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Code is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var dashboardSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Dashboard");
  if (!dashboardSheet) {
    dashboardSheet = ss.insertSheet("Dashboard");
    dashboardSheet.appendRow(["Code", "Description", "Roles", "Users"]);
  }
  
  var dashboardData = dashboardSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Find and update record
  var found = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < dashboardData.length; i++) {
    if (dashboardData[i][0] && dashboardData[i][0].toString() == code.toString()) {
      dashboardSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([[code, description, roles, users]]);
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!found) {
    // If not found, append as new row
    dashboardSheet.appendRow([code, description, roles, users]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Dashboard record updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// SALESMAN SALES SUMMARY HANDLER (DASHBOARD)
// ============================================

// Get sales summary grouped by salesman for the current month
function handleGetSalesmanSalesSummaryGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Calculate current month date range
  var now = new Date();
  var year = now.getFullYear();
  var month = now.getMonth(); // 0-based
  var firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
  var lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0); // Last day of current month
  
  var fromDate = Utilities.formatDate(firstDay, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  var toDate = Utilities.formatDate(lastDay, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  
  // Build salesman lookup map (code -> description)
  var salesmanSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Salesman");
  var salesmanData = salesmanSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var salesmanMap = {};
  for (var s = 1; s < salesmanData.length; s++) {
    if (salesmanData[s][0]) {
      salesmanMap[String(salesmanData[s][0]).toLowerCase()] = salesmanData[s][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Get all sales from SalesHeader sheet
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Aggregate sales by salesman
  var salesBySalesman = {};
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var dateObj = salesHeaderData[i][2] ? new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Filter by current month
      if (dateStr < fromDate || dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      var salesmanCode = String(salesHeaderData[i][18] || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!salesmanCode) continue;
      
      var grossAmount = parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][5]) || 0;
      var netAmount = parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][9]) || 0;
      
      if (!salesBySalesman[salesmanCode]) {
        salesBySalesman[salesmanCode] = {
          salesmanCode: salesmanCode,
          salesmanName: salesmanMap[salesmanCode] || salesmanCode,
          totalGross: 0,
          totalNet: 0,
          invoiceCount: 0
        };
      }
      
      salesBySalesman[salesmanCode].totalGross += grossAmount;
      salesBySalesman[salesmanCode].totalNet += netAmount;
      salesBySalesman[salesmanCode].invoiceCount++;
    }
  }
  
  // Convert to array and sort by totalGross descending
  var summary = [];
  for (var key in salesBySalesman) {
    if (salesBySalesman.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
      var item = salesBySalesman[key];
      item.totalGross = Math.round(item.totalGross * 100) / 100;
      item.totalNet = Math.round(item.totalNet * 100) / 100;
      summary.push(item);
    }
  }
  
  summary.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.totalGross - a.totalGross;
  });
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    summary: summary,
    month: month + 1,
    year: year,
    fromDate: fromDate,
    toDate: toDate
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// STOCK TRANSACTION POSTING HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Ensure StockTransaction sheet exists with proper headers
function ensureStockTransactionSheet(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("StockTransaction");
    sheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "Date", "PartyType", "PartyCode", "PartyDesc", "StockCode", "StockDesc", "Qty", "BaseUnit", "Rate", "Amount", "CostRate", "CostValue"]);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists with proper headers
function ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("AccountTransaction");
    sheet.appendRow(["DOCTYPE", "DOCNO", "DOCSRNO", "AC_DOCNO", "SUBLEDGER_DOCNO", "DOCDATE", "VALDATE", "NARRATION", "AMOUNT", "BAL_AMOUNT", "REF_NO", "REF_DATE", "CREATED_BY", "CREATED_TS"]);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Get system parameter value from SystemParameters sheet
function getSystemParameterValue(ss, paramCode, defaultValue) {
  if (defaultValue === undefined) defaultValue = '';
  var paramsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SystemParameters");
  if (!paramsSheet) return defaultValue;
  var paramsData = paramsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < paramsData.length; i++) {
    if (paramsData[i][0] && paramsData[i][0].toString() == paramCode.toString()) {
      return paramsData[i][1] || defaultValue;
    }
  }
  return defaultValue;
}

// Get stock transaction records
function handleGetStockTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (!stockTransactionSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", transactions: [] };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var data = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var transactions = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      transactions.push({
        docType: data[i][0] || '',
        docNo: data[i][1] || '',
        docSrNo: data[i][2] || 0,
        date: data[i][3] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][3]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        partyType: data[i][4] || '',
        partyCode: data[i][5] || '',
        partyDesc: data[i][6] || '',
        stockCode: data[i][7] || '',
        stockDesc: data[i][8] || '',
        qty: parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0,
        baseUnit: data[i][10] || '',
        rate: parseFloat(data[i][11]) || 0,
        amount: parseFloat(data[i][12]) || 0,
        costRate: parseFloat(data[i][13]) || 0,
        costValue: parseFloat(data[i][14]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", transactions: transactions };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get stock unit conversion factor
function getStockUnitConversion(ss, stockCode, fromUnit, toUnit) {
  // If same unit, no conversion needed
  if (fromUnit === toUnit) {
    return { factor: 1, fromQty: 1, toQty: 1 };
  }
  
  var stockUnitSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockUnit");
  if (!stockUnitSheet) {
    return { factor: 1, fromQty: 1, toQty: 1 };
  }
  
  var data = stockUnitSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Look for conversion from fromUnit to toUnit
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] && data[i][0].toString() == stockCode.toString()) {
      if (data[i][1] && data[i][1].toString() == fromUnit.toString()) {
        var fromQty = parseFloat(data[i][2]) || 1;
        var toQty = parseFloat(data[i][3]) || 1;
        return { factor: fromQty / toQty, fromQty: fromQty, toQty: toQty };
      }
    }
  }
  
  // No conversion found, assume 1:1
  return { factor: 1, fromQty: 1, toQty: 1 };
}

// ============================================
// STOCK BALANCE HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get stock balance (aggregated by StockCode from StockTransaction)
function handleGetStockBalanceGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (!stockTransactionSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", stockBalances: [], summary: { totalStocks: 0, totalQty: 0, totalCostValue: 0 } };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var data = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Aggregate balances by StockCode
  var balanceMap = {};
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][7]) { // Check if StockCode exists
      var stockCode = data[i][7].toString();
      var stockDesc = data[i][8] || '';
      var baseUnit = data[i][10] || '';
      var qty = parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0;
      var costRate = parseFloat(data[i][13]) || 0;
      var costValue = parseFloat(data[i][14]) || 0;
      
      // For outbound transactions (negative qty), CostValue represents COGS that left inventory
      // so we must subtract it from total cost to get remaining inventory value
      if (qty < 0) {
        // Sales/outbound: CostValue represents cost of goods sold - subtract from inventory
        costValue = -Math.abs(costValue);
      } else {
        // Inbound: CostValue adds to inventory
        costValue = Math.abs(costValue);
      }
      
      if (!balanceMap[stockCode]) {
        balanceMap[stockCode] = {
          stockCode: stockCode,
          stockDesc: stockDesc,
          balanceQty: 0,
          baseUnit: baseUnit,
          totalCostValue: 0,
          inboundQty: 0,
          inboundCostValue: 0,
          outboundQty: 0,
          outboundCostValue: 0
        };
      } else {
        // Update description/unit if later entries have more info
        if (stockDesc && !balanceMap[stockCode].stockDesc) {
          balanceMap[stockCode].stockDesc = stockDesc;
        }
        if (baseUnit && !balanceMap[stockCode].baseUnit) {
          balanceMap[stockCode].baseUnit = baseUnit;
        }
      }
      
      balanceMap[stockCode].balanceQty += qty;
      balanceMap[stockCode].totalCostValue += costValue;
      
      if (qty > 0) {
        balanceMap[stockCode].inboundQty += qty;
        balanceMap[stockCode].inboundCostValue += costValue;
      } else {
        balanceMap[stockCode].outboundQty += Math.abs(qty);
        balanceMap[stockCode].outboundCostValue += Math.abs(costValue);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Convert to array
  var stockBalances = [];
  for (var key in balanceMap) {
    if (balanceMap.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
      var item = balanceMap[key];
      // Apply search filter
      if (search) {
        if (item.stockCode.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            item.stockDesc.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      // Calculate average cost rate (only for items with non-zero balance)
      item.avgCostRate = item.balanceQty !== 0 ? Math.round((item.totalCostValue / item.balanceQty) * 100) / 100 : 0;
      item.balanceQty = Math.round(item.balanceQty * 100) / 100;
      item.totalCostValue = Math.round(item.totalCostValue * 100) / 100;
      stockBalances.push(item);
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by StockCode ascending
  stockBalances.sort(function(a, b) {
    return a.stockCode.localeCompare(b.stockCode);
  });
  
  // Calculate summary
  var totalStocks = stockBalances.length;
  var totalQty = 0;
  var totalCostValue = 0;
  
  for (var i = 0; i < stockBalances.length; i++) {
    totalQty += stockBalances[i].balanceQty;
    totalCostValue += stockBalances[i].totalCostValue;
  }
  
  totalQty = Math.round(totalQty * 100) / 100;
  totalCostValue = Math.round(totalCostValue * 100) / 100;
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    stockBalances: stockBalances,
    summary: {
      totalStocks: totalStocks,
      totalQty: totalQty,
      totalCostValue: totalCostValue
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// STOCK CARD HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get stock card (movement ledger for a specific stock item)
function handleGetStockCardGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  var stockCode = (e.parameter.stockCode || '').toString();
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';

  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);

  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }

  // Validate input
  if (!stockCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock code is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }

  // Get stock master info
  var stockMasterSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockInfo = null;
  if (stockMasterSheet) {
    var stockData = stockMasterSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
      if (stockData[i][0] && stockData[i][0].toString().toUpperCase() == stockCode.toUpperCase()) {
        stockInfo = {
          code: stockData[i][0].toString(),
          description: stockData[i][1] || '',
          baseUnit: stockData[i][2] || ''
        };
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!stockInfo) {
    // If not found in StockMaster, still allow viewing transactions using a minimal info object
    stockInfo = {
      code: stockCode,
      description: '',
      baseUnit: 'PCS'
    };
  }

  // Get transactions
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (!stockTransactionSheet) {
    var emptyResult = {
      status: "success",
      stockInfo: stockInfo,
      transactions: [],
      openingBalance: { qty: 0, costValue: 0 },
      summary: { totalInQty: 0, totalOutQty: 0, closingQty: 0, closingCostValue: 0 }
    };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }

  var data = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allTransactions = [];

  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][7] && data[i][7].toString().toUpperCase() == stockCode.toUpperCase()) {
      var transDate = data[i][3] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][3]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      allTransactions.push({
        docType: data[i][0] || '',
        docNo: data[i][1] || '',
        docSrNo: data[i][2] || 0,
        date: transDate,
        partyType: data[i][4] || '',
        partyCode: data[i][5] || '',
        partyDesc: data[i][6] || '',
        stockCode: data[i][7] || '',
        stockDesc: data[i][8] || '',
        qty: parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0,
        baseUnit: data[i][10] || '',
        rate: parseFloat(data[i][11]) || 0,
        amount: parseFloat(data[i][12]) || 0,
        costRate: parseFloat(data[i][13]) || 0,
        costValue: parseFloat(data[i][14]) || 0
      });
    }
  }

  // Sort by date ascending, then by DocNo ascending
  allTransactions.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);
    return a.docNo.localeCompare(b.docNo);
  });

  // Calculate opening balance (transactions before fromDate)
  var openingQty = 0;
  var openingCostValue = 0;
  var selectedTransactions = [];
  var totalInQty = 0;
  var totalOutQty = 0;

  for (var t = 0; t < allTransactions.length; t++) {
    var trans = allTransactions[t];
    
    if (fromDate && trans.date < fromDate) {
      // This transaction is before the fromDate, add to opening balance
      openingQty += trans.qty;
      openingCostValue += (trans.qty > 0 ? Math.abs(trans.costValue) : -Math.abs(trans.costValue));
    } else if (toDate && trans.date > toDate) {
      // This transaction is after the toDate, skip
      continue;
    } else {
      // This transaction is within the date range
      selectedTransactions.push(trans);
      if (trans.qty > 0) {
        totalInQty += trans.qty;
      } else {
        totalOutQty += Math.abs(trans.qty);
      }
    }
  }

  // Calculate running balance and add to each transaction
  var runningQty = openingQty;
  var runningCostValue = openingCostValue;
  
  // Update stockInfo description if it was empty but we found it in transactions
  if (!stockInfo.description && selectedTransactions.length > 0) {
    stockInfo.description = selectedTransactions[0].stockDesc || '';
  }

  for (var t = 0; t < selectedTransactions.length; t++) {
    runningQty += selectedTransactions[t].qty;
    if (selectedTransactions[t].qty > 0) {
      runningCostValue += Math.abs(selectedTransactions[t].costValue);
    } else {
      runningCostValue -= Math.abs(selectedTransactions[t].costValue);
    }
    selectedTransactions[t].runningQty = Math.round(runningQty * 100) / 100;
    selectedTransactions[t].runningCostValue = Math.round(runningCostValue * 100) / 100;

    // Separate in/out qty for display
    if (selectedTransactions[t].qty > 0) {
      selectedTransactions[t].inQty = Math.round(selectedTransactions[t].qty * 100) / 100;
      selectedTransactions[t].outQty = 0;
    } else {
      selectedTransactions[t].inQty = 0;
      selectedTransactions[t].outQty = Math.round(Math.abs(selectedTransactions[t].qty) * 100) / 100;
    }
  }

  var closingQty = Math.round(runningQty * 100) / 100;
  var closingCostValue = Math.round(runningCostValue * 100) / 100;

  var result = {
    status: "success",
    stockInfo: stockInfo,
    transactions: selectedTransactions,
    openingBalance: {
      qty: Math.round(openingQty * 100) / 100,
      costValue: Math.round(openingCostValue * 100) / 100
    },
    summary: {
      totalInQty: Math.round(totalInQty * 100) / 100,
      totalOutQty: Math.round(totalOutQty * 100) / 100,
      closingQty: closingQty,
      closingCostValue: closingCostValue
    }
  };

  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Calculate Moving Average Cost for a stock as of a given date
function calculateMovingAverageCost(ss, stockCode, asOfDate) {
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (!stockTransactionSheet) {
    return 0;
  }
  
  var data = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var totalQty = 0;
  var totalCostValue = 0;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][7] && data[i][7].toString() == stockCode.toString()) {
      var transDate = data[i][3] ? new Date(data[i][3]) : null;
      var transDateStr = transDate ? Utilities.formatDate(transDate, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Only include transactions on or before the asOfDate
      if (transDateStr <= asOfDate) {
        var qty = parseFloat(data[i][9]) || 0;
        var costValue = parseFloat(data[i][14]) || 0;
        totalQty += qty;
        totalCostValue += costValue;
      }
    }
  }
  
  if (totalQty === 0) return 0;
  return totalCostValue / totalQty;
}

// Post SOP to StockTransaction
function handlePostSOPToStockTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if already posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SOPHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][7] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SOP document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get SOP details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SOPDetails sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var sopDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      sopDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: parseFloat(detailsData[i][5]) || 0,
        unit: detailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: parseFloat(detailsData[i][7]) || 0,
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][8]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (sopDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get StockMaster to find base units
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet ? stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var stockMap = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0]) {
      stockMap[stockData[i][0].toString()] = {
        description: stockData[i][1] || '',
        baseUnit: stockData[i][2] || ''
      };
    }
  }
  
  // Ensure StockTransaction sheet exists
  var stockTransactionSheet = ensureStockTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  // Post each detail line to StockTransaction
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  for (var d = 0; d < sopDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = sopDetails[d];
    var stockCode = detail.stockCode;
    var stockDesc = detail.stockDescription;
    var docUnit = detail.unit;
    var docQty = detail.qty;
    var docRate = detail.rate;
    var docAmount = detail.amount;
    
    // Get base unit from StockMaster
    var stockInfo = stockMap[stockCode.toString()];
    var baseUnit = (stockInfo && stockInfo.baseUnit) || docUnit || 'PCS';
    
    // Convert to base unit if needed
    var baseQty = docQty;
    var baseRate = docRate;
    var baseAmount = docAmount;
    
    if (docUnit && docUnit !== baseUnit) {
      var conversion = getStockUnitConversion(ss, stockCode, docUnit, baseUnit);
      baseQty = docQty * conversion.factor;
      baseRate = docRate / conversion.factor;
      baseAmount = baseQty * baseRate;
    }
    
    // For SOP, CostRate = Rate (opening stock cost equals the rate)
    var costRate = baseRate;
    var costValue = baseQty * costRate;
    
    // Add to StockTransaction sheet
    stockTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'SOP',       // DocType
      docNo,       // DocNo
      detail.docSrNo, // DocSrNo
      headerDate,  // Date
      '',          // PartyType (empty for SOP)
      '',          // PartyCode (empty for SOP)
      '',          // PartyDesc (empty for SOP)
      stockCode,   // StockCode
      stockDesc,   // StockDesc
      baseQty,     // Qty (in base unit)
      baseUnit,    // BaseUnit
      baseRate,    // Rate (in base unit)
      baseAmount,  // Amount
      costRate,    // CostRate
      costValue    // CostValue
    ]);
  }
  
  // Update SOPHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 8).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 9).setValue(postedTime);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "SOP posted to stock transaction successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel SOP stock transaction post
function handleCancelSOPStockTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SOPHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SOPHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var postedBy = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      postedBy = headerData[i][7] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SOP document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is not posted" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete from StockTransaction sheet
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (stockTransactionSheet) {
    var transData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = transData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (transData[i][0] == 'SOP' && transData[i][1] == docNo) {
        stockTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in SOPHeader
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 8).setValue('');
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 9).setValue('');
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "SOP stock transaction post cancelled successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// PURCHASE INVOICE STOCK TRANSACTION POSTING
// ============================================

// Post PI to StockTransaction
function handlePostPIToStockTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if already posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "PurchaseHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  var supplierCode = '';
  var supplierDesc = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][19] || ''; // PostedBy at index 19 (column 20)
      supplierCode = headerData[i][3] || '';
      supplierDesc = headerData[i][4] || '';
      break;
    }
  }

  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Purchase document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get PI details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "PurchaseDetails sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var piDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      piDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: parseFloat(detailsData[i][5]) || 0,
        unit: detailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: parseFloat(detailsData[i][7]) || 0,
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][8]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (piDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get StockMaster to find base units
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet ? stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var stockMap = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0]) {
      stockMap[stockData[i][0].toString()] = {
        description: stockData[i][1] || '',
        baseUnit: stockData[i][2] || ''
      };
    }
  }
  
  // Ensure StockTransaction sheet exists
  var stockTransactionSheet = ensureStockTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  // Post each detail line to StockTransaction
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  for (var d = 0; d < piDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = piDetails[d];
    var stockCode = detail.stockCode;
    var stockDesc = detail.stockDescription;
    var docUnit = detail.unit;
    var docQty = detail.qty;
    var docRate = detail.rate;
    var docAmount = detail.amount;
    
    // Get base unit from StockMaster
    var stockInfo = stockMap[stockCode.toString()];
    var baseUnit = (stockInfo && stockInfo.baseUnit) || docUnit || 'PCS';
    
    // Convert to base unit if needed
    var baseQty = docQty;
    var baseRate = docRate;
    var baseAmount = docAmount;
    
    if (docUnit && docUnit !== baseUnit) {
      var conversion = getStockUnitConversion(ss, stockCode, docUnit, baseUnit);
      baseQty = docQty * conversion.factor;
      baseRate = docRate / conversion.factor;
      baseAmount = baseQty * baseRate;
    }
    
    // Calculate Moving Average Cost including this purchase
    // First, calculate the total cost value of this line
    var lineCostValue = baseQty * baseRate;
    
    // Get current moving average cost before this transaction
    var currentAvgCost = calculateMovingAverageCost(ss, stockCode, headerDate);
    
    // Calculate new moving average cost including this purchase
    // Get existing total qty and cost value for this stock
    var transData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var existingQty = 0;
    var existingCostValue = 0;
    for (var t = 1; t < transData.length; t++) {
      if (transData[t][7] && transData[t][7].toString() == stockCode.toString()) {
        var transDate = transData[t][3] ? new Date(transData[t][3]) : null;
        var transDateStr = transDate ? Utilities.formatDate(transDate, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
        if (transDateStr <= headerDate) {
          existingQty += parseFloat(transData[t][9]) || 0;
          existingCostValue += parseFloat(transData[t][14]) || 0;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // New moving average cost = (existing cost value + new line cost value) / (existing qty + new qty)
    var totalQty = existingQty + baseQty;
    var totalCostValue = existingCostValue + lineCostValue;
    var costRate = totalQty > 0 ? totalCostValue / totalQty : baseRate;
    var costValue = baseQty * costRate;
    
    // Add to StockTransaction sheet
    stockTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'PI',        // DocType
      docNo,       // DocNo
      detail.docSrNo, // DocSrNo
      headerDate,  // Date
      'AP',        // PartyType (Accounts Payable for Purchase)
      supplierCode, // PartyCode (Supplier code)
      supplierDesc, // PartyDesc (Supplier name)
      stockCode,   // StockCode
      stockDesc,   // StockDesc
      baseQty,     // Qty (in base unit)
      baseUnit,    // BaseUnit
      baseRate,    // Rate (in base unit)
      baseAmount,  // Amount
      costRate,    // CostRate (Moving Average Cost)
      costValue    // CostValue
    ]);
  }
  
  // ---- POST TO ACCOUNT TRANSACTION ----
  // Read header totals for accounting entries
  var grossAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][5]) || 0;
  var discountAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][7]) || 0;
  var vatAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][8]) || 0;
  var netAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][9]) || 0;
  var refNo = headerData[headerRow][16] || '';
  var refDate = headerData[headerRow][17] || '';
  
  // Get system parameters for account codes
  var purchaseAccount = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'PurchaseAccount', '5010101');
  var inputVATAccount = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'InputVATAccount', '2010811');
  var accountsPayableCode = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'AccountsPayableCode', 'AP');
  var purchaseDiscountAccount = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'PurchaseDiscountAccount', '23789');
  
  // Get formatted values
  var docDate = headerDate;
  var valDate = headerDate;
  var createdBy = validation.userName;
  var createdTs = postedTime;
  
  // Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  var docSrNo = 0;
  
  // For VAT Inclusive, the purchase amount is the taxable value (Net - VAT)
  // For VAT Exclusive, the purchase amount is the Gross value
  var rateType = headerData[headerRow][15] || '';
  var purchaseAmount = 0;
  if (rateType == 'Include') {
    // VAT Inclusive: Purchase = Net - VAT (the value excluding VAT)
    purchaseAmount = netAmount - vatAmount;
  } else {
    // VAT Exclusive: Purchase = Gross (full value, VAT on top)
    purchaseAmount = grossAmount;
  }
  
  // Line 1: Purchase (Stock) Account - Debit = -PurchaseAmount
  docSrNo++;
  accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
    'PI',                    // DOCTYPE
    docNo,                   // DOCNO
    docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
    purchaseAccount,         // AC_DOCNO (Purchase account)
    '',                      // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO (none for stock)
    docDate,                 // DOCDATE
    valDate,                 // VALDATE
    'Purchase',              // NARRATION
    -purchaseAmount,         // AMOUNT (negative = debit side)
    -purchaseAmount,         // BAL_AMOUNT
    supplierCode,            // REF_NO
    docDate,                 // REF_DATE
    createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
    createdTs                // CREATED_TS
  ]);
  
  // Line 2: Discount Account - Credit = +DiscountAmount (only if discount > 0)
  // For RateType = Include, the purchase amount already reflects the discount
  // (purchaseAmount = netAmount - vatAmount = discounted, VAT-exclusive value),
  // so posting discount separately would double-count it and cause imbalance.
  // For RateType = Exclude, posting discount separately is needed since
  // purchaseAmount = grossAmount (pre-discount value).
  if (discountAmount > 0 && rateType != 'Include') {
    docSrNo++;
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'PI',                    // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
      purchaseDiscountAccount, // AC_DOCNO (Discount account)
      '',                      // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      docDate,                 // DOCDATE
      valDate,                 // VALDATE
      'Purchase Discount',     // NARRATION
      discountAmount,          // AMOUNT (positive = credit side)
      discountAmount,          // BAL_AMOUNT
      supplierCode,            // REF_NO
      docDate,                 // REF_DATE
      createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
      createdTs                // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Line 3 (or 2 if no discount): VAT Account - Debit = -VAT (only if vat > 0)
  if (vatAmount > 0) {
    docSrNo++;
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'PI',                    // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
      inputVATAccount,         // AC_DOCNO (VAT Input account)
      '',                      // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      docDate,                 // DOCDATE
      valDate,                 // VALDATE
      'VAT',                   // NARRATION
      -vatAmount,              // AMOUNT (negative = debit side)
      -vatAmount,              // BAL_AMOUNT
      supplierCode,            // REF_NO
      docDate,                 // REF_DATE
      createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
      createdTs                // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Line 4 (or 3): Supplier (AP) Account - Credit = +Net
  docSrNo++;
  accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
    'PI',                    // DOCTYPE
    docNo,                   // DOCNO
    docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
    accountsPayableCode,     // AC_DOCNO (AP control account)
    supplierCode,            // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO (Supplier code)
    docDate,                 // DOCDATE
    valDate,                 // VALDATE
    'Supplier',              // NARRATION
    netAmount,               // AMOUNT (positive = credit side)
    netAmount,               // BAL_AMOUNT
    supplierCode,            // REF_NO
    docDate,                 // REF_DATE
    createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
    createdTs                // CREATED_TS
  ]);
  
  // Update PurchaseHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  // PurchaseHeader has 19 columns (indices 0-18), PostedBy at column 20 (index 19), PostedTime at column 21 (index 20)
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 20).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 21).setValue(postedTime);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Purchase invoice posted to stock and accounting successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime };

  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel PI stock transaction post
function handleCancelPIStockTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("PurchaseHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "PurchaseHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var postedBy = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      postedBy = headerData[i][19] || ''; // PostedBy at index 19 (column 20)
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Purchase document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is not posted" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }

  
  // Delete from StockTransaction sheet
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (stockTransactionSheet) {
    var transData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = transData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (transData[i][0] == 'PI' && transData[i][1] == docNo) {
        stockTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Delete from AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var acctData = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = acctData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (acctData[i][0] == 'PI' && acctData[i][1] == docNo) {
        accountTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in PurchaseHeader
  // PurchaseHeader has 19 columns (indices 0-18), PostedBy at column 20 (index 19), PostedTime at column 21 (index 20)
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 20).setValue('');
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 21).setValue('');
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Purchase invoice stock transaction post cancelled successfully" };

  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// SALES INVOICE STOCK TRANSACTION POSTING
// ============================================

// Post SI to StockTransaction
function handlePostSIToStockTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if already posted
