  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SalesHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  var customerCode = '';
  var customerDesc = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][19] || ''; // PostedBy at index 19 (column 20)
      customerCode = headerData[i][3] || '';
      customerDesc = headerData[i][4] || ''; // narration may also serve, but we look up customer desc
      break;
    }
  }

  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Sales document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Look up customer description from CustomerMaster
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  if (customerSheet && customerCode) {
    var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var c = 1; c < customerData.length; c++) {
      if (customerData[c][0] && customerData[c][0].toString() == customerCode.toString()) {
        customerDesc = customerData[c][1] || '';
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get SI details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SalesDetails sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var siDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      siDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: parseFloat(detailsData[i][5]) || 0,
        unit: detailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: parseFloat(detailsData[i][7]) || 0,
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][8]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (siDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get StockMaster to find base units
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet ? stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var stockMap = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0]) {
      stockMap[stockData[i][0].toString()] = {
        description: stockData[i][1] || '',
        baseUnit: stockData[i][2] || ''
      };
    }
  }
  
  // Ensure StockTransaction sheet exists
  var stockTransactionSheet = ensureStockTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  // Post each detail line to StockTransaction
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  for (var d = 0; d < siDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = siDetails[d];
    var stockCode = detail.stockCode;
    var stockDesc = detail.stockDescription;
    var docUnit = detail.unit;
    var docQty = detail.qty;
    var docRate = detail.rate;
    var docAmount = detail.amount;
    
    // Get base unit from StockMaster
    var stockInfo = stockMap[stockCode.toString()];
    var baseUnit = (stockInfo && stockInfo.baseUnit) || docUnit || 'PCS';
    
    // Convert to base unit if needed
    var baseQty = docQty;
    var baseRate = docRate;
    var baseAmount = docAmount;
    
    if (docUnit && docUnit !== baseUnit) {
      var conversion = getStockUnitConversion(ss, stockCode, docUnit, baseUnit);
      baseQty = docQty * conversion.factor;
      baseRate = docRate / conversion.factor;
      baseAmount = baseQty * baseRate;
    }
    
    // For SI (Sales), qty is negative (outflow)
    baseQty = -Math.abs(baseQty);
    
    // CostRate = Moving Average Cost (calculated before this SI since sales don't affect cost)
    var costRate = calculateMovingAverageCost(ss, stockCode, headerDate);
    var costValue = Math.abs(baseQty) * costRate;
    
    // Add to StockTransaction sheet
    stockTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'SI',        // DocType
      docNo,       // DocNo
      detail.docSrNo, // DocSrNo
      headerDate,  // Date
      'AR',        // PartyType (Accounts Receivable for Sales)
      customerCode, // PartyCode (Customer code)
      customerDesc, // PartyDesc (Customer name)
      stockCode,   // StockCode
      stockDesc,   // StockDesc
      baseQty,     // Qty (in base unit, negative for sales)
      baseUnit,    // BaseUnit
      baseRate,    // Rate (in base unit)
      baseAmount,  // Amount
      costRate,    // CostRate (Moving Average Cost)
      costValue    // CostValue
    ]);
  }
  
  // ---- POST TO ACCOUNT TRANSACTION ----
  // Read header totals for accounting entries
  var grossAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][5]) || 0;
  var discountAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][7]) || 0;
  var vatAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][8]) || 0;
  var netAmount = parseFloat(headerData[headerRow][9]) || 0;
  var refNo = headerData[headerRow][16] || '';
  var refDate = headerData[headerRow][17] || '';
  
  // Get system parameters for account codes
  var salesAccount = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'SalesAccount', '');
  var outputVATAccount = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'OutputVATAccount', '');
  var accountsReceivableCode = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'AccountsReceivableCode', '');
  var salesDiscountAccount = getSystemParameterValue(ss, 'SalesDiscountAccount', '');
  
  // Get formatted values
  var docDate = headerDate;
  var valDate = headerDate;
  var createdBy = validation.userName;
  var createdTs = postedTime;
  
  // Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  var docSrNo = 0;
  
  // Calculate accounting amounts based on RateType
  var rateType = headerData[headerRow][15] || '';
  var netAmt = netAmount || 0;
  var vatAmt = vatAmount || 0;
  var discAmt = discountAmount || 0;
  var grossAmt = grossAmount || 0;
  
  var arAmt, salesRevenue;
  var postDiscountSeparately = false;
  
  if (rateType === 'Exclude') {
    // Exclude: Net = Gross - Disc + VAT (VAT on top of pre-discount)
    // AR = Net (what customer owes after disc)
    // Revenue = Gross (full pre-discount sale value)
    // Discount posted separately
    arAmt = netAmt;
    salesRevenue = grossAmt;
    postDiscountSeparately = (discAmt > 0);
  } else {
    // Include: Net = (Gross - Disc) with VAT inside, so AR discounted already
    // AR = Net (discounted amount including VAT)
    // Revenue = Net - VAT (discounted, VAT-exclusive revenue)
    // Discount NOT posted separately (already reflected in AR/Revenue)
    arAmt = netAmt;
    salesRevenue = netAmt - vatAmt;
    postDiscountSeparately = false; // Discount already factored into net
  }
  
  // Entry 1: Debit Accounts Receivable (Customer) - Negative amount
  docSrNo++;
  accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
    'SI',                    // DOCTYPE
    docNo,                   // DOCNO
    docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
    accountsReceivableCode,  // AC_DOCNO (AR account)
    customerCode,            // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO (Customer code)
    docDate,                 // DOCDATE
    valDate,                 // VALDATE
    'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Customer', // NARRATION
    -arAmt,                  // AMOUNT (negative = debit side)
    -arAmt,                  // BAL_AMOUNT
    customerCode,            // REF_NO
    docDate,                 // REF_DATE
    createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
    createdTs                // CREATED_TS
  ]);
  
  // Entry 2: Credit Sales Account (Revenue) - Positive amount
  docSrNo++;
  accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
    'SI',                    // DOCTYPE
    docNo,                   // DOCNO
    docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
    salesAccount,            // AC_DOCNO (Sales account)
    '',                      // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
    docDate,                 // DOCDATE
    valDate,                 // VALDATE
    'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Sales Revenue', // NARRATION
    salesRevenue,            // AMOUNT (positive = credit side)
    salesRevenue,            // BAL_AMOUNT
    customerCode,            // REF_NO
    docDate,                 // REF_DATE
    createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
    createdTs                // CREATED_TS
  ]);
  
  // Entry 3: Credit Output VAT Account - Positive amount (if VAT > 0)
  if (vatAmt > 0 && outputVATAccount) {
    docSrNo++;
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'SI',                    // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
      outputVATAccount,        // AC_DOCNO (Output VAT account)
      '',                      // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      docDate,                 // DOCDATE
      valDate,                 // VALDATE
      'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Output VAT', // NARRATION
      vatAmt,                  // AMOUNT (positive = credit side)
      vatAmt,                  // BAL_AMOUNT
      customerCode,            // REF_NO
      docDate,                 // REF_DATE
      createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
      createdTs                // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Entry 4: Debit Sales Discount Account - Negative amount (only for Exclude)
  if (postDiscountSeparately && salesDiscountAccount) {
    docSrNo++;
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'SI',                    // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      docSrNo,                 // DOCSRNO
      salesDiscountAccount,    // AC_DOCNO (Sales Discount account)
      '',                      // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      docDate,                 // DOCDATE
      valDate,                 // VALDATE
      'Sale ' + docNo + ' - Discount', // NARRATION
      -discAmt,                // AMOUNT (negative = debit side)
      -discAmt,                // BAL_AMOUNT
      customerCode,            // REF_NO
      docDate,                 // REF_DATE
      createdBy,               // CREATED_BY
      createdTs                // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Update SalesHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  // SalesHeader has 19 columns (indices 0-18), PostedBy at column 20 (index 19), PostedTime at column 21 (index 20)
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 20).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 21).setValue(postedTime);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Sales invoice posted to stock and accounting successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime };

  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel SI stock transaction post
function handleCancelSIStockTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "SalesHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var postedBy = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      postedBy = headerData[i][19] || ''; // PostedBy at index 19 (column 20)
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Sales document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is not posted" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }

  // Delete from StockTransaction sheet
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (stockTransactionSheet) {
    var transData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = transData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (transData[i][0] == 'SI' && transData[i][1] == docNo) {
        stockTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Delete from AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var acctData = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = acctData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (acctData[i][0] == 'SI' && acctData[i][1] == docNo) {
        accountTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in SalesHeader
  // SalesHeader has 19 columns (indices 0-18), PostedBy at column 20 (index 19), PostedTime at column 21 (index 20)
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 20).setValue('');
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 21).setValue('');
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Sales invoice post cancelled successfully" };

  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// STOCK ADJUSTMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all stock adjustment records
function handleGetStockAdjustmentsGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all records from ADJHeader sheet
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", stockAdjustments: [], totalRecords: 0, totalPages: 0, currentPage: page, pageSize: pageSize };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allRecords = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var narration = headerData[i][4] || '';
      var date = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply search filter if search term is provided
      if (search) {
        if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      
      allRecords.push({
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: date,
        amount: headerData[i][3] || 0,
        narration: narration,
        createdBy: headerData[i][5] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][6] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][7] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][8] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo
  allRecords.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allRecords.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var records = allRecords.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    stockAdjustments: records, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single stock adjustment by DocNo
function handleGetStockAdjustmentByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock adjustment record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: headerData[i][1],
        date: headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        amount: headerData[i][3] || 0,
        narration: headerData[i][4] || '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][5] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][6] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][7] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][8] || ''
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock adjustment record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet ? detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: detailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: detailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: detailsData[i][5] || 0,
        unit: detailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: detailsData[i][7] || 0,
        amount: detailsData[i][8] || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", stockAdjustment: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new stock adjustment
function handleCreateStockAdjustmentGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var stockAdjustmentDataJson = e.parameter.stockAdjustmentData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse data
  var stockAdjustmentData;
  try {
    stockAdjustmentData = JSON.parse(stockAdjustmentDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!stockAdjustmentData.header || !stockAdjustmentData.details || stockAdjustmentData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    headerSheet = ss.insertSheet("ADJHeader");
    headerSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "Date", "Amount", "Narration", "CreatedBy", "CreatedTime", "PostedBy", "PostedTime"]);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 10000;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('ADJ')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(3));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'ADJ' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = stockAdjustmentData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  headerSheet.appendRow([
    'ADJ', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.amount || 0,
    header.narration || '',
    validation.userName,
    createdTime
  ]);
  
  // Add details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("ADJDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "StockCode", "StockDescription", "Qty", "Unit", "Rate", "Amount"]);
  }
  for (var i = 0; i < stockAdjustmentData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = stockAdjustmentData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'ADJ', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.amount || 0
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock adjustment created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing stock adjustment
function handleUpdateStockAdjustmentGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var stockAdjustmentDataJson = e.parameter.stockAdjustmentData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse data
  var stockAdjustmentData;
  try {
    stockAdjustmentData = JSON.parse(stockAdjustmentDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!stockAdjustmentData.header || !stockAdjustmentData.header.docNo || !stockAdjustmentData.details || stockAdjustmentData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = stockAdjustmentData.header.docNo;
  
  // Update header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock adjustment record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      var header = stockAdjustmentData.header;
      headerSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 7).setValues([[
        'ADJ', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.amount || 0,
        header.narration || '',
        headerData[i][5],
        headerData[i][6]
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock adjustment record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  } else {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("ADJDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "StockCode", "StockDescription", "Qty", "Unit", "Rate", "Amount"]);
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < stockAdjustmentData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = stockAdjustmentData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'ADJ', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.stockCode,
      detail.stockDescription || '',
      detail.qty || 0,
      detail.unit || '',
      detail.rate || 0,
      detail.amount || 0
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock adjustment updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete stock adjustment
function handleDeleteStockAdjustmentGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock adjustment record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Stock adjustment record not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Stock adjustment deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Post ADJ to StockTransaction
function handlePostADJToStockTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if already posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "ADJHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][7] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "ADJ document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get ADJ details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "ADJDetails sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var adjDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      adjDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        stockCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        stockDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        qty: parseFloat(detailsData[i][5]) || 0,
        unit: detailsData[i][6] || '',
        rate: parseFloat(detailsData[i][7]) || 0,
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][8]) || 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (adjDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get StockMaster to find base units
  var stockSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockMaster");
  var stockData = stockSheet ? stockSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var stockMap = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < stockData.length; i++) {
    if (stockData[i][0]) {
      stockMap[stockData[i][0].toString()] = {
        description: stockData[i][1] || '',
        baseUnit: stockData[i][2] || ''
      };
    }
  }
  
  // Ensure StockTransaction sheet exists
  var stockTransactionSheet = ensureStockTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  // Post each detail line to StockTransaction
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  for (var d = 0; d < adjDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = adjDetails[d];
    var stockCode = detail.stockCode;
    var stockDesc = detail.stockDescription;
    var docUnit = detail.unit;
    var docQty = detail.qty;
    var docRate = detail.rate;
    var docAmount = detail.amount;
    
    // Get base unit from StockMaster
    var stockInfo = stockMap[stockCode.toString()];
    var baseUnit = (stockInfo && stockInfo.baseUnit) || docUnit || 'PCS';
    
    // Convert to base unit if needed
    var baseQty = docQty;
    var baseRate = docRate;
    var baseAmount = docAmount;
    
    if (docUnit && docUnit !== baseUnit) {
      var conversion = getStockUnitConversion(ss, stockCode, docUnit, baseUnit);
      baseQty = docQty * conversion.factor;
      baseRate = docRate / conversion.factor;
      baseAmount = baseQty * baseRate;
    }
    
    // For ADJ, CostRate = Rate (adjustment cost equals the rate)
    var costRate = baseRate;
    var costValue = Math.abs(baseQty) * costRate;
    
    // Add to StockTransaction sheet (qty preserves sign - positive for Up, negative for Down)
    stockTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'ADJ',       // DocType
      docNo,       // DocNo
      detail.docSrNo, // DocSrNo
      headerDate,  // Date
      '',          // PartyType (empty for ADJ)
      '',          // PartyCode (empty for ADJ)
      '',          // PartyDesc (empty for ADJ)
      stockCode,   // StockCode
      stockDesc,   // StockDesc
      baseQty,     // Qty (in base unit, preserves sign)
      baseUnit,    // BaseUnit
      baseRate,    // Rate (in base unit)
      baseAmount,  // Amount
      costRate,    // CostRate
      costValue    // CostValue
    ]);
  }
  
  // Update ADJHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 8).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 9).setValue(postedTime);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "ADJ posted to stock transaction successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel ADJ stock transaction post
function handleCancelADJStockTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if posted
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("ADJHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "ADJHeader sheet not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var postedBy = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      postedBy = headerData[i][7] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "ADJ document not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Document is not posted" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete from StockTransaction sheet
  var stockTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("StockTransaction");
  if (stockTransactionSheet) {
    var transData = stockTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = transData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (transData[i][0] == 'ADJ' && transData[i][1] == docNo) {
        stockTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in ADJHeader
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 8).setValue('');
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 9).setValue('');
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "ADJ stock transaction post cancelled successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// JOURNAL VOUCHER MANAGEMENT HANDLERS
// ============================================

// Get all journal vouchers (with server-side pagination)
function handleGetJournalVouchersGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read pagination and search parameters
  var page = parseInt(e.parameter.page) || 1;
  var pageSize = parseInt(e.parameter.pageSize) || 10;
  var search = (e.parameter.search || '').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all vouchers from JournalVoucherHeader sheet
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    // Create sheet if it doesn't exist
    headerSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherHeader");
    headerSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "Date", "Narration", "Debit", "Credit", "RefNo", "RefDate", "CreatedBy", "CreatedTime", "PostedBy", "PostedTime"]);
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", jv: [], totalRecords: 0, totalPages: 0, currentPage: page, pageSize: pageSize };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allVouchers = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var narration = headerData[i][3] || '';
      var date = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply search filter if search term is provided
      if (search) {
        if (docNo.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            narration.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1 &&
            date.toLowerCase().indexOf(search) === -1) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      
      allVouchers.push({
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: date,
        narration: narration,
        debit: parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0,
        credit: parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort descending by DocNo (most recent/last document first)
  allVouchers.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  var totalRecords = allVouchers.length;
  var totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize);
  
  var startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
  var endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, totalRecords);
  var vouchers = allVouchers.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: vouchers, 
    totalRecords: totalRecords,
    totalPages: totalPages,
    currentPage: page,
    pageSize: pageSize
  };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Get single journal voucher by DocNo
function handleGetJournalVoucherByDocNoGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Journal voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var header = null;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      header = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: headerData[i][1],
        date: headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        narration: headerData[i][3] || '',
        debit: parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0,
        credit: parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      };
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!header) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Journal voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet ? detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
  var details = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      details.push({
        docType: detailsData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: detailsData[i][1],
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        acCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        acDescription: detailsData[i][4] || '',
        subledger: detailsData[i][5] || '',
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][6]) || 0,
        narration: detailsData[i][7] || '',
        mode: detailsData[i][8] || '',
        bank: detailsData[i][9] || '',
        instrumentNo: detailsData[i][10] || '',
        instrumentDate: detailsData[i][11] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(detailsData[i][11]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", jv: { header: header, details: details } };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Create new journal voucher
function handleCreateJournalVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var jvDataJson = e.parameter.jvData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse journal voucher data
  var jvData;
  try {
    jvData = JSON.parse(jvDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid journal voucher data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!jvData.header || !jvData.details || jvData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate debit = credit balance
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < jvData.details.length; d++) {
    var amt = parseFloat(jvData.details[d].amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) {
      totalDebit += Math.abs(amt);
    } else {
      totalCredit += amt;
    }
  }
  
  if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.01) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Voucher is not balanced! Total Debit must equal Total Credit." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get or create JournalVoucherHeader sheet
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    headerSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherHeader");
    headerSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "Date", "Narration", "Debit", "Credit", "RefNo", "RefDate", "CreatedBy", "CreatedTime", "PostedBy", "PostedTime"]);
  }
  
  // Generate DocNo - starting from 1000 per sample
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var maxNum = 1000; // Starting number
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) {
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      if (docNo.startsWith('JV')) {
        var num = parseInt(docNo.substring(2));
        if (num > maxNum) {
          maxNum = num;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  var newDocNo = 'JV' + (maxNum + 1);
  
  // Add header
  var header = jvData.header;
  var now = new Date();
  var createdTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  headerSheet.appendRow([
    'JV', // DocType
    newDocNo,
    header.date,
    header.narration || '',
    totalDebit,
    totalCredit,
    header.refNo || '',
    header.refDate || '',
    validation.userName,
    createdTime,
    '', // PostedBy
    ''  // PostedTime
  ]);
  
  // Get or create JournalVoucherDetails sheet
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (!detailsSheet) {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "ACCode", "ACDescription", "Subledger", "Amount", "Narration", "Mode", "Bank", "InstrumentNo", "InstrumentDate"]);
  }
  
  // Add details
  for (var i = 0; i < jvData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = jvData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'JV', // DocType
      newDocNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.acCode,
      detail.acDescription || '',
      detail.subledger || '',
      parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0,
      detail.narration || '',
      detail.mode || '',
      detail.bank || '',
      detail.instrumentNo || '',
      detail.instrumentDate || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Journal Voucher created successfully", docNo: newDocNo };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Update existing journal voucher
function handleUpdateJournalVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var jvDataJson = e.parameter.jvData;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Parse journal voucher data
  var jvData;
  try {
    jvData = JSON.parse(jvDataJson);
  } catch (error) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid journal voucher data format" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Validate input
  if (!jvData.header || !jvData.header.docNo || !jvData.details || jvData.details.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Header with DocNo and at least one detail line are required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  var docNo = jvData.header.docNo;
  
  // Validate debit = credit balance
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  for (var d = 0; d < jvData.details.length; d++) {
    var amt = parseFloat(jvData.details[d].amount) || 0;
    if (amt < 0) {
      totalDebit += Math.abs(amt);
    } else {
      totalCredit += amt;
    }
  }
  
  if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.01) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Voucher is not balanced! Total Debit must equal Total Credit." };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Update header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      // Check if posted
      if (headerData[i][10] && headerData[i][10] !== '') {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot update a posted journal voucher. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      
      var header = jvData.header;
      headerSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 12).setValues([[
        'JV', // DocType
        docNo,
        header.date,
        header.narration || '',
        totalDebit,
        totalCredit,
        header.refNo || '',
        header.refDate || '',
        headerData[i][8], // Keep original CreatedBy
        headerData[i][9], // Keep original CreatedTime
        '', // PostedBy
        ''  // PostedTime
      ]]);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Journal voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete existing details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  } else {
    detailsSheet = ss.insertSheet("JournalVoucherDetails");
    detailsSheet.appendRow(["DocType", "DocNo", "DocSrNo", "ACCode", "ACDescription", "Subledger", "Amount", "Narration", "Mode", "Bank", "InstrumentNo", "InstrumentDate"]);
  }
  
  // Add new details
  for (var i = 0; i < jvData.details.length; i++) {
    var detail = jvData.details[i];
    detailsSheet.appendRow([
      'JV', // DocType
      docNo,
      detail.docSrNo || (i + 1),
      detail.acCode,
      detail.acDescription || '',
      detail.subledger || '',
      parseFloat(detail.amount) || 0,
      detail.narration || '',
      detail.mode || '',
      detail.bank || '',
      detail.instrumentNo || '',
      detail.instrumentDate || ''
    ]);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Journal Voucher updated successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Delete journal voucher
function handleDeleteJournalVoucherGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerFound = false;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      // Check if posted
      if (headerData[i][10] && headerData[i][10] !== '') {
        var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Cannot delete a posted journal voucher. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
      }
      headerSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      headerFound = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (!headerFound) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Journal voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Delete details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  if (detailsSheet) {
    var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = detailsData.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
        detailsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Journal Voucher deleted successfully" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Post JV to AccountTransaction
function handlePostJVToAccountTransactionGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Get voucher header
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headerRow = -1;
  var headerDate = '';
  var postedBy = '';
  var headerRefNo = '';
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
      headerRow = i;
      headerDate = headerData[i][2] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][2]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      postedBy = headerData[i][10] || '';
      headerRefNo = headerData[i][6] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  
  if (headerRow === -1) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Journal voucher not found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (postedBy) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Journal voucher is already posted by " + postedBy };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Check if accounting entries already exist
  var existingSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (existingSheet) {
    var existingData = existingSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < existingData.length; i++) {
      if (existingData[i][0] && existingData[i][0].toString() == 'JV' && existingData[i][1] && existingData[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        var alreadyResult = { status: "error", message: "Accounting entries already exist for this document. Cancel post first." };
        if (callback) {
          return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(alreadyResult) + ")")
            .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
        }
        return createJsonResponse(alreadyResult);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get voucher details
  var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
  var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var jvDetails = [];
  
  for (var i = 1; i < detailsData.length; i++) {
    if (detailsData[i][1] == docNo) {
      jvDetails.push({
        docSrNo: detailsData[i][2] || 0,
        acCode: detailsData[i][3] || '',
        subledger: detailsData[i][5] || '',
        amount: parseFloat(detailsData[i][6]) || 0,
        narration: detailsData[i][7] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  
  if (jvDetails.length === 0) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "No details found for this document" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Ensure AccountTransaction sheet exists
  var accountTransactionSheet = ensureAccountTransactionSheet(ss);
  
  var now = new Date();
  var postedTime = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
  
  // Post each detail line to AccountTransaction
  for (var d = 0; d < jvDetails.length; d++) {
    var detail = jvDetails[d];
    var amount = detail.amount;
    
    accountTransactionSheet.appendRow([
      'JV',                    // DOCTYPE
      docNo,                   // DOCNO
      detail.docSrNo,          // DOCSRNO
      detail.acCode,           // AC_DOCNO
      detail.subledger,        // SUBLEDGER_DOCNO
      headerDate,              // DOCDATE
      headerDate,              // VALDATE
      detail.narration || 'Journal Voucher ' + docNo, // NARRATION
      amount,                  // AMOUNT (negative = debit, positive = credit)
      amount,                  // BAL_AMOUNT
      headerRefNo,             // REF_NO
      headerDate,              // REF_DATE
      validation.userName,     // CREATED_BY
      postedTime               // CREATED_TS
    ]);
  }
  
  // Update JournalVoucherHeader with PostedBy and PostedTime
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 11).setValue(validation.userName);
  headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 12).setValue(postedTime);
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "Journal Voucher posted to accounting successfully", postedBy: validation.userName, postedTime: postedTime };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// Cancel JV AccountTransaction Post (Delete entries)
function handleCancelJVAccountTransactionPostGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var docNo = e.parameter.docNo;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!docNo) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "DocNo is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Find header row for clearing posted status
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  var headerRow = -1;
  if (headerSheet) {
    var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
      if (headerData[i][1] == docNo) {
        headerRow = i;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Delete from AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (accountTransactionSheet) {
    var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var deleted = false;
    
    for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (data[i][0] && data[i][0].toString() == 'JV' && data[i][1] && data[i][1].toString() == docNo.toString()) {
        accountTransactionSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        deleted = true;
      }
    }
    
    // Clear PostedBy and PostedTime in JournalVoucherHeader
    if (headerRow !== -1 && deleted) {
      headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 11).setValue('');
      headerSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 12).setValue('');
    }
    
    var result = { status: "success", message: deleted ? "Accounting entries cancelled successfully" : "No accounting entries found" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(result);
  }
  
  var result = { status: "success", message: "No accounting entries found" };
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// JOURNAL VOUCHER REPORT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get journal voucher report with date range filters
function handleGetJournalVoucherReportGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Read filter parameters
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var reportType = (e.parameter.reportType || 'headerwise').toLowerCase();
  
  // Get all journal vouchers from JournalVoucherHeader sheet
  var headerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherHeader");
  if (!headerSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { status: "success", jv: [], summary: { totalCount: 0, totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0 } };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var headerData = headerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allVouchers = [];
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  
  for (var i = 1; i < headerData.length; i++) {
    if (headerData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var docNo = headerData[i][1].toString();
      var dateObj = headerData[i][2] ? new Date(headerData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Apply date range filter
      if (fromDate && dateStr < fromDate) continue;
      if (toDate && dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      var debit = parseFloat(headerData[i][4]) || 0;
      var credit = parseFloat(headerData[i][5]) || 0;
      totalDebit += debit;
      totalCredit += credit;
      
      var voucher = {
        docType: headerData[i][0] || '',
        docNo: docNo,
        date: dateStr,
        narration: headerData[i][3] || '',
        debit: debit,
        credit: credit,
        refNo: headerData[i][6] || '',
        refDate: headerData[i][7] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(headerData[i][7]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '',
        createdBy: headerData[i][8] || '',
        createdTime: headerData[i][9] || '',
        postedBy: headerData[i][10] || '',
        postedTime: headerData[i][11] || ''
      };
      
      allVouchers.push(voucher);
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date descending, then by DocNo descending
  allVouchers.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return b.date.localeCompare(a.date);
    return b.docNo.localeCompare(a.docNo);
  });
  
  // If reportType is linewise, fetch detail lines for each voucher
  if (reportType === 'linewise') {
    var detailsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("JournalVoucherDetails");
    if (detailsSheet) {
      var detailsData = detailsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      
      // Build a map of docNo -> details array
      var detailsMap = {};
      for (var d = 1; d < detailsData.length; d++) {
        var detailDocNo = detailsData[d][1];
        if (detailDocNo) {
          if (!detailsMap[detailDocNo]) {
            detailsMap[detailDocNo] = [];
          }
          var amount = parseFloat(detailsData[d][6]) || 0;
          detailsMap[detailDocNo].push({
            docSrNo: detailsData[d][2] || 0,
            acCode: detailsData[d][3] || '',
            acDescription: detailsData[d][4] || '',
            subledger: detailsData[d][5] || '',
            amount: amount,
            debit: amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0,
            credit: amount > 0 ? amount : 0,
            narration: detailsData[d][7] || '',
            mode: detailsData[d][8] || ''
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Attach details to each voucher
      for (var vi = 0; vi < allVouchers.length; vi++) {
        allVouchers[vi].details = detailsMap[allVouchers[vi].docNo] || [];
      }
    }
  }
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    jv: allVouchers,
    reportType: reportType,
    summary: {
      totalCount: allVouchers.length,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// STATEMENT OF ACCOUNT HANDLER
// ============================================

// Get ledger/statement of account (by GL account, optionally filtered by party for AR/AP)
function handleGetStatementOfAccountGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var acCode = e.parameter.acCode || '';
  var partyType = e.parameter.partyType || '';
  var partyCode = e.parameter.partyCode || '';
  var fromDate = e.parameter.fromDate || '';
  var toDate = e.parameter.toDate || '';
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
