    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  if (!acCode) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Account code is required" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Look up account description from COA
  var accountDescription = acCode;
  var coaSheet = ss.getSheetByName("COA");
  var coaDescMap = {};
  if (coaSheet) {
    var coaData = coaSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < coaData.length; i++) {
      var acType = String(coaData[i][1] || '');
      var accountCode = String(coaData[i][0] || '');
      var desc = coaData[i][2] || '';
      if (accountCode) {
        coaDescMap[accountCode] = desc;
        if (accountCode === acCode) {
          accountDescription = desc;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Look up party name from CustomerMaster or SupplierMaster (if partyCode provided)
  var partyName = partyCode;
  if (partyCode) {
    if (partyType === 'Customer') {
      var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
      if (customerSheet) {
        var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (var c = 1; c < customerData.length; c++) {
          if (customerData[c][0] && String(customerData[c][0]).toUpperCase() == String(partyCode).toUpperCase()) {
            partyName = customerData[c][1] || partyCode;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    } else {
      var supplierSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SupplierMaster");
      if (supplierSheet) {
        var supplierData = supplierSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (var s = 1; s < supplierData.length; s++) {
          if (supplierData[s][0] && String(supplierData[s][0]).toUpperCase() == String(partyCode).toUpperCase()) {
            partyName = supplierData[s][1] || partyCode;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Get AccountTransaction sheet
  var accountTransactionSheet = ss.getSheetByName("AccountTransaction");
  if (!accountTransactionSheet) {
    var emptyResult = { 
      status: "success", 
      account: { code: acCode, description: accountDescription },
      party: partyCode ? { type: partyType, code: partyCode, name: partyName } : null,
      entries: [], 
      summary: { openingBalance: 0, totalDebit: 0, totalCredit: 0, closingBalance: 0 } 
    };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(emptyResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(emptyResult);
  }
  
  var data = accountTransactionSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var allEntries = [];
  var openingBalance = 0;
  var totalDebit = 0;
  var totalCredit = 0;
  
  // Collect all entries for this account (and party if provided) across all dates first
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var accountCode = String(data[i][3] || '');
    if (accountCode === acCode) {
      // If partyCode provided, filter by subledger too
      if (partyCode) {
        var subledger = String(data[i][4] || '');
        if (subledger !== partyCode) {
          continue;
        }
      }
      
      var valDate = data[i][6] ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][6]), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      var amount = parseFloat(data[i][8]) || 0;
      var docType = String(data[i][0] || '');
      var partySubledger = String(data[i][4] || '');
      
      // If before fromDate, add to opening balance
      if (fromDate && valDate < fromDate) {
        openingBalance += amount;
        continue;
      }
      
      // Skip entries after toDate
      if (toDate && valDate > toDate) {
        continue;
      }
      
      // Add to totals
      if (amount < 0) {
        totalDebit += Math.abs(amount);
      } else {
        totalCredit += amount;
      }
      
      allEntries.push({
        docType: docType,
        docNo: String(data[i][1] || ''),
        acDocNo: accountCode,
        accountDescription: coaDescMap[accountCode] || '',
        subledger: partySubledger,
        date: valDate,
        narration: data[i][7] || '',
        amount: amount,
        debit: amount < 0 ? Math.abs(amount) : 0,
        credit: amount > 0 ? amount : 0
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by date ascending, then by docNo ascending
  allEntries.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.date !== b.date) return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);
    return a.docNo.localeCompare(b.docNo);
  });
  
  // Calculate running balance for each entry
  var runningBalance = openingBalance;
  for (var j = 0; j < allEntries.length; j++) {
    runningBalance += allEntries[j].amount;
    allEntries[j].runningBalance = Math.round(runningBalance * 100) / 100;
  }
  
  var closingBalance = runningBalance;
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    account: { code: acCode, description: accountDescription },
    party: partyCode ? { type: partyType, code: partyCode, name: partyName } : null,
    entries: allEntries,
    summary: {
      openingBalance: Math.round(openingBalance * 100) / 100,
      totalDebit: Math.round(totalDebit * 100) / 100,
      totalCredit: Math.round(totalCredit * 100) / 100,
      closingBalance: Math.round(closingBalance * 100) / 100
    }
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}

// ============================================
// CUSTOMER SALES SUMMARY HANDLER (DASHBOARD)
// ============================================


// Get sales summary grouped by customer for the current month
function handleGetCustomerSalesSummaryGet(e) {
  var sessionId = e.parameter.sessionId;
  var userId = e.parameter.userId;
  var callback = e.parameter.callback;
  var ipAddress = getClientIP(e);
  
  // Validate session
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  var validation = validateSession(ss, sessionId, userId, ipAddress);
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    var errorResult = { status: "error", message: "Invalid or expired session" };
    if (callback) {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(errorResult) + ")")
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
    }
    return createJsonResponse(errorResult);
  }
  
  // Calculate current month date range
  var now = new Date();
  var year = now.getFullYear();
  var month = now.getMonth(); // 0-based
  var firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
  var lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0); // Last day of current month
  
  var fromDate = Utilities.formatDate(firstDay, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  var toDate = Utilities.formatDate(lastDay, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
  
  // Build customer lookup map (code -> description)
  var customerSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CustomerMaster");
  var customerData = customerSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var customerMap = {};
  for (var c = 1; c < customerData.length; c++) {
    if (customerData[c][0]) {
      customerMap[String(customerData[c][0]).toLowerCase()] = customerData[c][1] || '';
    }
  }
  
  // Get all sales from SalesHeader sheet
  var salesHeaderSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SalesHeader");
  var salesHeaderData = salesHeaderSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  
  // Aggregate sales by customer
  var salesByCustomer = {};
  
  for (var i = 1; i < salesHeaderData.length; i++) {
    if (salesHeaderData[i][1]) { // Check if DocNo exists
      var dateObj = salesHeaderData[i][2] ? new Date(salesHeaderData[i][2]) : null;
      var dateStr = dateObj ? Utilities.formatDate(dateObj, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : '';
      
      // Filter by current month
      if (dateStr < fromDate || dateStr > toDate) continue;
      
      var customerCode = String(salesHeaderData[i][3] || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!customerCode) continue;
      
      var grossAmount = parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][5]) || 0;
      var netAmount = parseFloat(salesHeaderData[i][9]) || 0;
      
      if (!salesByCustomer[customerCode]) {
        salesByCustomer[customerCode] = {
          customerCode: customerCode,
          customerName: customerMap[customerCode] || customerCode,
          totalGross: 0,
          totalNet: 0,
          invoiceCount: 0
        };
      }
      
      salesByCustomer[customerCode].totalGross += grossAmount;
      salesByCustomer[customerCode].totalNet += netAmount;
      salesByCustomer[customerCode].invoiceCount++;
    }
  }
  
  // Convert to array and sort by totalGross descending
  var summary = [];
  for (var key in salesByCustomer) {
    if (salesByCustomer.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
      var item = salesByCustomer[key];
      item.totalGross = Math.round(item.totalGross * 100) / 100;
      item.totalNet = Math.round(item.totalNet * 100) / 100;
      summary.push(item);
    }
  }
  
  summary.sort(function(a, b) {
    return b.totalGross - a.totalGross;
  });
  
  var result = { 
    status: "success", 
    summary: summary,
    month: month + 1,
    year: year,
    fromDate: fromDate,
    toDate: toDate
  };
  
  if (callback) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(callback + "(" + JSON.stringify(result) + ")")
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JAVASCRIPT);
  }
  return createJsonResponse(result);
}
